# FILE: `directory\templates\directory\index.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}The Exchange Directory — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<!-- Optional external link if you move styles back to disk later -->
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/directory.css' %}">

<!-- =========================================================
     EMBEDDED ARCHITECTURAL STYLES
========================================================= -->
<style>
    :root {
        --black:        #0a0a0a;
        --white:        #ffffff;
        --off-white:    #f5f4f1;
        --orange:       #ff5a1f;
        --grey:         #6b6b6b;
        --grey-line:    rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.1);

        --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
        --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;

        /* Thematic Duotones */
        --material-a:   #1c2b45;
        --material-b:   #3f5c8a;
        --edible-a:     #1f3a2a;
        --edible-b:     #4f7a52;
    }

    * { box-sizing: border-box; }

    .directory-page {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        color: var(--black);
        background: var(--white);
    }

    /* Shared Typography & Utilities */
    .eyebrow {
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        letter-spacing: 0.22em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--grey);
        display: block;
        margin-bottom: 14px;
    }
    .eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

    .section-title {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: clamp(2.2rem, 4.5vw, 3.4rem);
        line-height: 1.15;
        text-align: center;
        margin: 0 0 24px;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    .lede {
        font-size: 1.05rem;
        color: var(--grey);
        max-width: 760px;
        line-height: 1.65;
    }
    .lede.center { margin: 0 auto; text-align: center; }

    .btn-outline {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        border: 1px solid var(--black);
        color: var(--black);
        background: transparent;
        padding: 12px 26px;
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 0.16em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: all 0.25s ease;
        text-decoration: none;
    }
    .btn-outline:hover {
        background: var(--orange);
        border-color: var(--orange);
        color: var(--white);
    }

    .ambient-microcopy {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 6px;
        margin-top: 36px;
    }
    .ambient-microcopy.center { align-items: center; text-align: center; }
    .ambient-microcopy span {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-style: italic;
        font-size: 0.95rem;
        color: var(--grey);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       1. INTRO SECTION
    ========================================================= */
    .directory-intro {
        padding: 90px 40px 70px;
        max-width: 1000px;
        margin: 0 auto;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    }

    .stats-row {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 80px;
        margin-top: 50px;
        padding-top: 40px;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    }
    .stat { text-align: center; }
    .stat-num {
        display: block;
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 3rem;
        color: var(--orange);
        line-height: 1;
    }
    .stat-label {
        display: block;
        margin-top: 8px;
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 0.16em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       2. HOW IT WORKS
    ========================================================= */
    .how-it-works {
        padding: 80px 40px 90px;
        max-width: 1280px;
        margin: 0 auto;
    }

    .steps-row {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        gap: 1px;
        background: var(--grey-line);
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        margin-bottom: 50px;
    }
    .step {
        background: var(--white);
        padding: 44px 34px;
        text-align: center;
        transition: background 0.25s ease;
    }
    .step:hover { background: var(--off-white); }
    .step-num {
        display: block;
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 2rem;
        color: var(--orange);
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }
    .step p { margin: 0; color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.95rem; line-height: 1.5; }

    .audience-label { margin-top: 40px; }
    .audience-tags {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 10px;
    }
    .audience-tags li {
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        background: var(--off-white);
        padding: 8px 18px;
        font-size: 0.78rem;
        font-weight: 500;
        letter-spacing: 0.08em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       3. UPGRADED LOOPS DETAIL (DASHBOARD MODULES)
    ========================================================= */
    .loops-detail {
        padding: 40px 40px 100px;
        max-width: 1280px;
        margin: 0 auto;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 80px;
    }

    .loop-module {
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        background: var(--grey-line);
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 1px;
    }

    /* Header Bar */
    .loop-header {
        padding: 60px 50px;
        color: var(--white);
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: flex-start;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
    }
    .loop-module.material .loop-header {
        background: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--material-a), var(--material-b));
        border-top: 4px solid #5b8dd9;
    }
    .loop-module.edible .loop-header {
        background: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--edible-a), var(--edible-b));
        border-top: 4px solid #4caf81;
    }

    .loop-header .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); margin-bottom: 8px; }
    .loop-header h2 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: clamp(2rem, 3.5vw, 2.8rem);
        line-height: 1.15;
        margin: 0 0 16px;
        max-width: 700px;
        color: var(--white);
    }
    .loop-header .lede { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); max-width: 700px; margin: 0; }

    /* 3-Column Content Grid */
    .loop-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1.2fr 1fr 1fr;
        gap: 1px;
        background: var(--grey-line);
        width: 100%;
    }

    .loop-card {
        background: var(--white);
        padding: 40px 34px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
    }
    .loop-card h4 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        margin: 0 0 20px;
        color: var(--black);
        padding-bottom: 12px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    }

    .scannable-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
    .scannable-list li {
        padding: 12px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(10,10,10,0.06);
        font-size: 0.88rem;
        line-height: 1.5;
        color: var(--grey);
    }
    .scannable-list li:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
    .scannable-list li strong { color: var(--black); font-weight: 600; display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; }

    /* Action Hub Inside Loop */
    .action-hub {
        background: var(--off-white);
        padding: 40px 34px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 24px;
    }
    .action-hub h4 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        margin: 0;
        color: var(--black);
    }
    .action-buttons {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 10px;
    }
    .action-buttons .btn-outline {
        width: 100%;
        background: var(--white);
        border-color: var(--grey-line);
    }
    .action-buttons .btn-outline:hover {
        background: var(--orange);
        border-color: var(--orange);
        color: var(--white);
    }
    .action-buttons .btn-outline.primary {
        background: var(--black);
        color: var(--white);
        border-color: var(--black);
    }
    .action-buttons .btn-outline.primary:hover {
        background: var(--orange);
        border-color: var(--orange);
    }

    .live-feed {
        margin-top: 14px;
        padding-top: 18px;
        border-top: 1px solid rgba(10,10,10,0.08);
    }
    .live-feed span { font-size: 0.7rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.12em; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 8px; font-weight: 600; }
    .live-feed ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
    .live-feed li { font-size: 0.82rem; color: var(--black); padding: 6px 0; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }

    /* =========================================================
       4. UPGRADED QUICK ACTIONS (4-COLUMN CARD STRIP)
    ========================================================= */
    .directory-actions {
        background: var(--black);
        color: var(--white);
        padding: 90px 40px;
        border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
    }

    .directory-actions .section-title { color: var(--white); margin-bottom: 50px; }

    .actions-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
        gap: 1px;
        background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
        border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
        max-width: 1280px;
        margin: 0 auto;
    }

    .action-card {
        background: #111111;
        padding: 44px 30px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        min-height: 280px;
        text-decoration: none;
        color: var(--white);
        transition: background 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
    }
    .action-card::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
        height: 3px;
        background: transparent;
        transition: background 0.3s ease, height 0.3s ease;
    }
    .action-card:hover {
        background: #181818;
        transform: translateY(-4px);
    }
    .action-card:hover::before {
        background: var(--orange);
        height: 4px;
    }

    .action-card .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); margin-bottom: 12px; }
    .action-card h3 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 1.6rem;
        line-height: 1.2;
        margin: 0 0 12px;
        color: var(--white);
    }
    .action-card p {
        font-size: 0.85rem;
        color: rgba(255,255,255,0.65);
        line-height: 1.5;
        margin: 0 0 24px;
    }

    .action-card .arrow-link {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 8px;
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 0.14em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--white);
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-bottom: 2px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
        width: fit-content;
        transition: color 0.25s ease, border-color 0.25s ease;
    }
    .action-card:hover .arrow-link {
        color: var(--orange);
        border-bottom-color: var(--orange);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       5. ROLE INFO
    ========================================================= */
    .directory-info {
        padding: 90px 40px 110px;
        max-width: 860px;
        margin: 0 auto;
    }
    .info-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
    .info-list li {
        padding: 16px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        font-size: 0.95rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--grey);
    }
    .info-list li strong { color: var(--black); font-weight: 600; }
    .info-more { color: var(--grey); margin-top: 30px; font-size: 0.95rem; }
    .info-more a { color: var(--orange); font-weight: 500; text-decoration: underline; }

    /* =========================================================
       RESPONSIVE BREAKPOINTS
    ========================================================= */
    @media (max-width: 1024px) {
        .loop-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
        .action-hub { grid-column: 1 / -1; }
        .actions-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr); }
    }

    @media (max-width: 768px) {
        .directory-intro, .how-it-works, .loops-detail, .directory-actions, .directory-info {
            padding-left: 20px;
            padding-right: 20px;
        }
        .stats-row { flex-direction: column; gap: 30px; }
        .steps-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
        .loop-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
        .actions-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
        .loop-header { padding: 40px 24px; }
    }
</style>

<div class="directory-page">

    <!-- 1. INTRO -->
    <section class="directory-intro">
        <span class="eyebrow center">The Exchange Directory</span>
        <h1 class="section-title">Surplus, matched with skill, creativity, and need.</h1>
        <p class="lede center">
            Every material. Every ingredient. Every overlooked resource — a second life waiting for its match.
            The Exchange Directory connects businesses with dormant stock, makers with imagination, and
            communities with value that already exists. This is where circularity becomes infrastructure.
        </p>
        <div class="ambient-microcopy center">
            <span>&ldquo;Nothing is waste until someone decides it is.&rdquo;</span>
            <span>&ldquo;Everything circulates.&rdquo;</span>
            <span>&ldquo;Surplus becomes starting points.&rdquo;</span>
            <span>&ldquo;Creativity is a form of logistics.&rdquo;</span>
        </div>

        {% if surplus_count is not None or demand_count is not None %}
        <div class="stats-row">
            <div class="stat">
                <span class="stat-num">{{ surplus_count|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="stat-label">Surplus Listings</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat">
                <span class="stat-num">{{ demand_count|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="stat-label">Demand Requests</span>
            </div>
        </div>
        {% endif %}
    </section>

    <!-- 2. HOW IT WORKS -->
    <section class="how-it-works">
        <span class="eyebrow center">How It Works</span>
        <h2 class="section-title">Three Simple Actions</h2>

        <div class="steps-row">
            <div class="step">
                <span class="step-num">01</span>
                <p>List surplus materials, architectural offcuts, or dormant stock.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="step">
                <span class="step-num">02</span>
                <p>Match with creators, chefs, makers, or forward-thinking businesses.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="step">
                <span class="step-num">03</span>
                <p>Create new value — installations, products, culture, or civic nourishment.</p>
            </div>
        </div>

        <span class="eyebrow center audience-label">Open To</span>
        <ul class="audience-tags">
            <li>Businesses</li>
            <li>Creators &amp; Makers</li>
            <li>Chefs &amp; Food Innovators</li>
            <li>Schools &amp; Universities</li>
            <li>Charities</li>
            <li>Sponsors &amp; Commissioners</li>
            <li>Councils &amp; Public Bodies</li>
        </ul>
    </section>

    <!-- 3. UPGRADED LOOPS DETAIL (DASHBOARD MODULES) -->
    <section class="loops-detail">

        <!-- MATERIAL LOOP MODULE -->
        <article class="loop-module material">
            <header class="loop-header">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">The Material Loop</span>
                    <h2>Surplus becomes structure, substance, and new cultural value.</h2>
                </div>
                <p class="lede">
                    Transforming business surplus into creative potential and economic gain. Materials are treated as data, story, and infrastructure for an imaginative economy. Nothing is static. Everything circulates.
                </p>
            </header>

            <div class="loop-grid">
                <!-- Col 1: Pathways -->
                <div class="loop-card">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Pathways &amp; Outcomes</h4>
                        <ul class="scannable-list">
                            <li><strong>Creative Reuse &amp; Fabrication</strong> Installations, artworks, prototypes, furniture, and spatial elements.</li>
                            <li><strong>Industrial Reprocessing</strong> Reprocessed materials, internal reuse streams, and cost-saving alternatives.</li>
                            <li><strong>Education &amp; Workshops</strong> Youth programmes, corporate team days, and skill-building labs.</li>
                            <li><strong>Material Intelligence</strong> University R&amp;D, product design studies, and material testing.</li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Col 2: Who It's For -->
                <div class="loop-card">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Target Ecosystem</h4>
                        <ul class="scannable-list">
                            <li><strong>Businesses</strong> Reduce waste overheads and improve measurable ESG metrics.</li>
                            <li><strong>Creators &amp; Makers</strong> Source affordable materials and prototype scalable ideas.</li>
                            <li><strong>Built Environment Teams</strong> Access unique finishes and offcuts for architectural builds.</li>
                            <li><strong>Councils &amp; Sponsors</strong> Fund and monitor regional circular economy impact.</li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Col 3: Action Hub & Live Feed -->
                <div class="action-hub">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Material Hub</h4>
                        <div class="action-buttons">
                            <a href="{% url 'directory:demand_list' %}" class="btn-outline primary">Explore Material Loop</a>
                            <a href="{% url 'directory:demand_create' %}" class="btn-outline">Add Your Demands</a>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {% if latest_surpluses %}
                    <div class="live-feed">
                        <span>Recently Listed</span>
                        <ul>
                            {% for s in latest_surpluses|slice:":3" %}
                                <li><strong>{{ s.company }}</strong> — {{ s.get_material_type_display }}</li>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                    {% endif %}
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>

        <!-- EDIBLE LOOP MODULE -->
        <article class="loop-module edible">
            <header class="loop-header">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">The Edible Loop</span>
                    <h2>Food becomes flavour, fuel, material, and future infrastructure.</h2>
                </div>
                <p class="lede">
                    Treating food as material intelligence capable of nourishment, creativity, bio-production, and scientific transformation. Nothing is "just waste." Everything has a next life.
                </p>
            </header>

            <div class="loop-grid">
                <!-- Col 1: Pathways -->
                <div class="loop-card">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Pathways &amp; Outcomes</h4>
                        <ul class="scannable-list">
                            <li><strong>Culinary Play &amp; Pop-ups</strong> Experimental menus, food art, pop-up kitchens, and edible installations.</li>
                            <li><strong>Civic Nourishment</strong> Safe community meals, cooking literacy workshops, and youth access.</li>
                            <li><strong>Biomaterial Innovation</strong> Bioplastics, organic dyes, natural pigments, compost, and fermentation substrates.</li>
                            <li><strong>Circular Research</strong> Experimental kitchens prototyping sustainable packaging and food futures.</li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Col 2: Who It's For -->
                <div class="loop-card">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Target Ecosystem</h4>
                        <ul class="scannable-list">
                            <li><strong>Food Businesses</strong> Save disposal costs and support ethical, circular redistribution.</li>
                            <li><strong>Chefs &amp; Creatives</strong> Source experimental ingredients and collaborative R&amp;D space.</li>
                            <li><strong>Community Partners</strong> Secure consistent ingredients for teaching, cooking, and preserving.</li>
                            <li><strong>Scientific Researchers</strong> Experiment with biomaterial extraction and waste transformation.</li>
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Col 3: Action Hub & Live Feed -->
                <div class="action-hub">
                    <div>
                        <h4>Edible Hub</h4>
                        <div class="action-buttons">
                            <a href="{% url 'directory:surplus_list' %}" class="btn-outline primary">Explore Edible Loop</a>
                            <a href="{% url 'directory:surplus_create' %}" class="btn-outline">Add Food Surplus</a>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {% if latest_demands %}
                    <div class="live-feed">
                        <span>Recently Requested</span>
                        <ul>
                            {% for d in latest_demands|slice:":3" %}
                                <li><strong>{{ d.organisation|default:"Anonymous" }}</strong> — {{ d.get_material_wanted_display }}</li>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </ul>
                    </div>
                    {% endif %}
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>

    </section>

    <!-- 4. UPGRADED QUICK ACTIONS (4-COLUMN CARD STRIP) -->
    <section class="directory-actions">
        <span class="eyebrow center">Ecosystem Entry Points</span>
        <h2 class="section-title">Take Immediate Action</h2>

        <div class="actions-grid">
            <a href="{% url 'directory:demand_list' %}" class="action-card">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">For Businesses</span>
                    <h3>List Material</h3>
                    <p>Turn dormant stock, offcuts, and corporate surplus into environmental impact.</p>
                </div>
                <span class="arrow-link">Start Listing <span>&rarr;</span></span>
            </a>

            <a href="{% url 'directory:demand_create' %}" class="action-card">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">For Creators</span>
                    <h3>Request Food / Stock</h3>
                    <p>Submit a demand request for ingredients or materials needed for your next build.</p>
                </div>
                <span class="arrow-link">Submit Request <span>&rarr;</span></span>
            </a>

            <a href="{% url 'website:impact_tracker' %}" class="action-card">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">Telemetry</span>
                    <h3>Track Impact</h3>
                    <p>Explore the Call Soso Forest and see how circular exchanges grow into lasting culture.</p>
                </div>
                <span class="arrow-link">View Forest <span>&rarr;</span></span>
            </a>

            <a href="{% url 'website:about' %}" class="action-card">
                <div>
                    <span class="eyebrow">Partnership</span>
                    <h3>Tiers &amp; Pricing</h3>
                    <p>Discover corporate membership tiers, circular audits, and custom activation pricing.</p>
                </div>
                <span class="arrow-link">Explore Tiers <span>&rarr;</span></span>
            </a>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- 5. ROLE INFO -->
    <section class="directory-info">
        <span class="eyebrow center">Who the Directory Is For</span>
        <ul class="info-list">
            <li><strong>Businesses</strong> — Turn surplus into ESG wins, reduce waste overheads, and commission meaningful regional work.</li>
            <li><strong>Creators &amp; Makers</strong> — Source materials, prototype ideas, craft new installations, and collaborate with industry.</li>
            <li><strong>Chefs &amp; Food Innovators</strong> — Access surplus ingredients, circular culinary briefs, and experimental food system labs.</li>
            <li><strong>Schools &amp; Universities</strong> — List or source material surplus for creative, scientific, and educational use.</li>
            <li><strong>Charities &amp; Civic Groups</strong> — Sourcing support for community workshops, nourishment, and local activations.</li>
            <li><strong>Sponsors &amp; Councils</strong> — Fund targeted circular projects and monitor measurable, localized environmental impact.</li>
        </ul>
        <p class="info-more center">Learn more about Call Soso's foundational ethos and cultural logic <a href="{% url 'website:about' %}">here</a>.</p>
```
```html
    </section>

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Surplus Listings — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/surplus.css' %}">

<div class="form-page">

    <section class="form-header">
        <p class="eyebrow center">The Material Loop</p>
        <h1 class="section-title">My Surplus Listings</h1>
        <p class="lede center">Manage your materials and share them with our creative ecosystem.</p>
        <div class="center">
            <a href="{% url 'directory:surplus_create' %}" class="btn-outline">Add New Surplus</a>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class="search-filters">
        <form method="GET" class="filter-form">
            <input type="text" name="q" placeholder="Search materials..." value="{{ query|default:'' }}">

            <select name="material_type">
                <option value="">All Types</option>
                <option value="wood" {% if material_type == 'wood' %}selected{% endif %}>Wood</option>
                <option value="metal" {% if material_type == 'metal' %}selected{% endif %}>Metal</option>
                <option value="textiles" {% if material_type == 'textiles' %}selected{% endif %}>Textiles</option>
            </select>

            <select name="location">
                <option value="">All Locations</option>
                <option value="Milton Keynes" {% if location == 'Milton Keynes' %}selected{% endif %}>Milton Keynes</option>
                <option value="London" {% if location == 'London' %}selected{% endif %}>London</option>
            </select>

            <button type="submit" class="btn-outline">Search</button>
        </form>
    </section>

    {% if surpluses %}
        <ul class="listing-rows">
            {% for surplus in surpluses %}
                <li>
                    <strong>{{ surplus.company }}</strong><br>
                    Material: {{ surplus.get_material_type_display }}<br>
                    Volume: {{ surplus.monthly_volume }} units<br>
                    Location: {{ surplus.location }}
                </li>
            {% endfor %}
        </ul>
    {% else %}
        <div class="empty-state">No surplus listings found.</div>
    {% endif %}

    <div class="form-actions center">
        <a href="{% url 'directory:directory_home' %}" class="btn-outline muted">Back to Directory Home</a>
    </div>

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

```python
from django.urls import path
from . import views

app_name = "directory"

urlpatterns = [
    path('', views.index, name='directory_home'),  # Main dashboard

    # Surplus
    path('surplus/', views.surplus_list, name='surplus_list'),
    path('surplus/create/', views.surplus_create, name='surplus_create'),

    # Demand
    path('demand/', views.demand_list, name='demand_list'),
    path('demand/create/', views.demand_create, name='demand_create'),

    # Matches
    path('matches/', views.match_list, name='match_list'),
    path('match/suggest/<int:surplus_id>/<int:demand_id>/', views.suggest_match, name='suggest_match'),
]
```

# FILE: `directory\views.py`

```python
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.db.models import Q
from .models import SurplusListing, DemandListing, Match
from .forms import SurplusListingForm, DemandListingForm
from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.conf import settings

# ===============================
# Directory Homepage
# ===============================
@login_required
def index(request):
    """
    Directory landing page.
    Shows counts for Material & Edible Loops.
    """
    surplus_count = SurplusListing.objects.count()
    demand_count = DemandListing.objects.count()
    # Optionally preview latest items
    latest_surpluses = SurplusListing.objects.order_by('-created_on')[:5]
    latest_demands = DemandListing.objects.order_by('-created_on')[:5]

    return render(request, 'directory/index.html', {
        'surplus_count': surplus_count,
        'demand_count': demand_count,
        'latest_surpluses': latest_surpluses,
        'latest_demands': latest_demands,
    })


# ===============================
# Surplus Listings & Create
# ===============================
@login_required
def surplus_list(request):
    """
    List the logged-in user's surplus listings with search & filters.
    """
    query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    material_type = request.GET.get('material_type', '')
    location = request.GET.get('location', '')

    listings = SurplusListing.objects.filter(user=request.user)

    if query:
        listings = listings.filter(
            Q(company__icontains=query) |
            Q(material_type__icontains=query)
        )
    if material_type:
        listings = listings.filter(material_type=material_type)
    if location:
        listings = listings.filter(location__icontains=location)

    return render(request, 'directory/surplus_list.html', {
        'surpluses': listings,
        'query': query,
        'material_type': material_type,
        'location': location,
    })


@login_required
def surplus_create(request):
    """
    Create a new surplus listing.
    """
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = SurplusListingForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
        if form.is_valid():
            listing = form.save(commit=False)
            listing.user = request.user
            listing.save()
            return redirect('directory:surplus_list')  # FIXED: was missing the 'directory:' namespace
    else:
        form = SurplusListingForm()
    return render(request, 'directory/surplus_form.html', {'form': form})


# ===============================
# Demand Listings & Create
# ===============================
@login_required
def demand_list(request):
    """
    List the logged-in user's demand listings with search & filters.
    """
    query = request.GET.get('q', '')
    material_wanted = request.GET.get('material_wanted', '')
    location = request.GET.get('location', '')

    listings = DemandListing.objects.filter(user=request.user)

    if query:
        listings = listings.filter(
            Q(organisation__icontains=query) |  # FIXED: model field is 'organisation', not 'org'
            Q(material_wanted__icontains=query)
        )
    if material_wanted:
        listings = listings.filter(material_wanted=material_wanted)
    if location:
        listings = listings.filter(location__icontains=location)

    return render(request, 'directory/demand_list.html', {
        'demands': listings,
        'query': query,
        'material_wanted': material_wanted,
        'location': location,
    })


@login_required
def demand_create(request):
    """
    Create a new demand listing.
    """
    if request.method == 'POST':
        form = DemandListingForm(request.POST, request.FILES)
        if form.is_valid():
            listing = form.save(commit=False)
            listing.user = request.user
            listing.save()
            return redirect('directory:demand_list')  # FIXED: was missing the 'directory:' namespace
    else:
        form = DemandListingForm()
    return render(request, 'directory/demand_form.html', {'form': form})


# ===============================
# Matches
# ===============================
@login_required
def suggest_match(request, surplus_id, demand_id):
    """
    Suggest a match between a surplus and a demand.
    Sends notification emails.
    """
    surplus = get_object_or_404(SurplusListing, pk=surplus_id)
    demand = get_object_or_404(DemandListing, pk=demand_id)
    Match.objects.create(surplus=surplus, demand=demand, suggested_by=request.user)

    # Send notification emails (silent fail)
    send_mail(
        subject="Call Soso: Potential Match Found!",
        message=f"A match has been suggested between surplus from {surplus.company} and demand from {demand.organisation}.",  # FIXED: was demand.org
        from_email=settings.DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
        recipient_list=[surplus.contact_email, demand.user.email],
        fail_silently=True,
    )
    return redirect('directory:match_list')  # FIXED: was missing the 'directory:' namespace


@login_required
def match_list(request):
    """
    List matches visible to the user.
    Admins see all matches.
    """
    if request.user.is_staff:
        matches = Match.objects.all()
    else:
        matches = Match.objects.filter(
            Q(surplus__user=request.user) | Q(demand__user=request.user)
        )
    return render(request, 'directory/match_list.html', {'matches': matches})
```

# FILE: `static\css\demand.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — DEMAND (FORMS & LISTINGS)
========================================================= */
:root {
    --black:     #0a0a0a;
    --white:     #ffffff;
    --off-white: #f5f4f1;
    --orange:    #ff5a1f;
    --grey:      #6b6b6b;
    --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

    --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.form-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 760px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 100px 20px; }

.center { text-align: center; }

.eyebrow {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.22em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--grey);
    display: block;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem);
    text-align: center;
    margin: 0 0 16px;
}

.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; margin: 0 auto 30px; max-width: 560px; }

.form-header { margin-bottom: 50px; }

.btn-outline {
    display: inline-block;
    border: 1px solid var(--black);
    color: var(--black);
    background: transparent;
    padding: 12px 26px;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.muted { border-color: var(--grey-line); color: var(--grey); }
.btn-outline.muted:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }

/* =========================================================
   FORM
========================================================= */
.site-form { border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding: 40px; }

.form-row { margin-bottom: 22px; }
.form-row label {
    display: block;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--grey);
    margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.site-form input,
.site-form select,
.site-form textarea {
    width: 100%;
    padding: 12px 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    background: var(--white);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    color: var(--black);
    outline: none;
}
.site-form input:focus,
.site-form select:focus,
.site-form textarea:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

.form-help { font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--grey); margin: 6px 0 0; }
.form-error { font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--orange); margin: 6px 0 0; }

.form-actions { display: flex; gap: 14px; margin-top: 30px; }
.form-actions.center { justify-content: center; }

/* =========================================================
   SEARCH & FILTERS
========================================================= */
.search-filters { margin: 40px 0; }
.filter-form {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 12px;
    justify-content: center;
}
.filter-form input,
.filter-form select {
    padding: 11px 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    min-width: 180px;
    flex: 1 1 200px;
    outline: none;
}
.filter-form input:focus,
.filter-form select:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
   LISTINGS
========================================================= */
.listing-rows { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 30px 0; }
.listing-rows li {
    padding: 18px 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    color: var(--grey);
}
.listing-rows li strong { color: var(--black); }
.listing-rows li .muted { color: var(--grey); }

.empty-state {
    text-align: center;
    padding: 50px 20px;
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    color: var(--grey);
    margin: 30px 0;
}

/* =========================================================
   RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 600px) {
    .site-form { padding: 26px; }
    .form-actions { flex-direction: column; }
}
```

# FILE: `static\css\directory.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — DIRECTORY
   Shares the token system used across the site.
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:        #0a0a0a;
  --white:        #ffffff;
  --off-white:    #f5f4f1;
  --orange:       #ff5a1f;
  --grey:         #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line:    rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;

  --material-a: #1c2b45;
  --material-b: #3f5c8a;
  --edible-a:   #1f3a2a;
  --edible-b:   #4f7a52;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.directory-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); }

.eyebrow {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem);
  text-align: center;
  margin: 0 0 26px;
}

.lede {
  font-size: 1rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  max-width: 760px;
  line-height: 1.65;
}
.lede.center { margin: 0 auto; text-align: center; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 12px 26px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }

.ambient-microcopy {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 30px;
}
.ambient-microcopy.center { align-items: center; text-align: center; }
.ambient-microcopy span {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: var(--grey);
}

.hairline-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
.hairline-list li {
  padding: 14px 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  font-size: 0.92rem;
  line-height: 1.55;
  color: var(--grey);
}
.hairline-list li strong { color: var(--black); }

.two-col {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 50px;
  margin: 34px 0;
}
.two-col h4 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  margin: 0 0 8px;
}

/* =========================================================
 INTRO
========================================================= */
.directory-intro { padding: 100px 40px 70px; max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto; }

.stats-row {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 60px;
  margin-top: 40px;
}
.stat { text-align: center; }
.stat-num {
  display: block;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 2.4rem;
  color: var(--orange);
  line-height: 1;
}
.stat-label {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 6px;
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
}

.latest-preview { margin: 10px 0 30px; max-width: 700px; margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto; }
.latest-preview h4 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.05rem;
  text-align: center;
  margin: 0 0 6px;
  color: var(--grey);
}
.latest-preview .hairline-list li { text-align: center; font-size: 0.85rem; padding: 10px 0; }

/* =========================================================
 HOW IT WORKS
========================================================= */
.how-it-works { padding: 60px 40px 100px; max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; }

.steps-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 60px;
}
.step { background: var(--white); padding: 34px; text-align: center; }
.step-num {
  display: block;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 1.6rem;
  color: var(--orange);
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}
.step p { margin: 0; color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.95rem; }

.audience-label { margin-top: 40px; }
.audience-tags {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 12px;
}
.audience-tags li {
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  padding: 8px 18px;
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  color: var(--grey);
}

/* =========================================================
 LOOPS DETAIL
========================================================= */
.loops-detail { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }

.loop-detail { border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); }

.loop-banner {
  padding: 70px 40px;
  text-align: center;
  color: var(--white);
}
.loop-detail.material .loop-banner { background: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--material-a), var(--material-b)); }
.loop-detail.edible .loop-banner   { background: linear-gradient(160deg, var(--edible-a), var(--edible-b)); }
.loop-banner .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.loop-banner h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 3vw, 2.2rem);
  max-width: 700px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

.loop-body { padding: 60px 40px; max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; }
.loop-body .lede { margin: 0 auto 10px; text-align: center; max-width: 800px; }

.loop-cta {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 14px;
  margin: 30px 0 10px;
}

/* =========================================================
 QUICK ACTIONS
========================================================= */
.directory-actions { padding: 80px 40px; text-align: center; background: var(--black); }
.directory-actions .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); }
.actions-grid {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 16px;
}
.directory-actions .btn-outline { border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); color: var(--white); }
.directory-actions .btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 ROLE INFO
========================================================= */
.directory-info { padding: 90px 40px; max-width: 800px; margin: 0 auto; }
.info-more { color: var(--grey); margin-top: 24px; }
.info-more a { color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .two-col { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 30px; }
  .steps-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}
```

# FILE: `static\css\surplus.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — SURPLUS (FORMS & LISTINGS)
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.form-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 760px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 100px 20px; }

.center { text-align: center; }

.eyebrow {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.6vw, 2.4rem);
  text-align: center;
  margin: 0 0 16px;
}

.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; margin: 0 auto 20px; max-width: 560px; }

.form-header { margin-bottom: 50px; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 12px 26px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.muted { border-color: var(--grey-line); color: var(--grey); }
.btn-outline.muted:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }

/* =========================================================
 FORM
 Django's {{ form.as_p }} renders <p><label/><input/></p> —
 styled generically here since there are no custom widget classes.
========================================================= */
.site-form { border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding: 40px; }

.site-form p { margin: 0 0 22px; }
.site-form label {
  display: block;
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.site-form input,
.site-form select,
.site-form textarea {
  width: 100%;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  color: var(--black);
  outline: none;
}
.site-form input:focus,
.site-form select:focus,
.site-form textarea:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

.form-actions { display: flex; gap: 14px; margin-top: 10px; }
.form-actions.center { justify-content: center; margin-top: 30px; }

/* =========================================================
 SEARCH & FILTERS
========================================================= */
.search-filters { margin: 40px 0; }
.filter-form {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  justify-content: center;
}
.filter-form input,
.filter-form select {
  padding: 11px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  min-width: 180px;
  flex: 1 1 200px;
  outline: none;
}
.filter-form input:focus,
.filter-form select:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 LISTINGS
========================================================= */
.listing-rows { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 30px 0; }
.listing-rows li {
  padding: 18px 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  line-height: 1.6;
}
.listing-rows li strong { color: var(--black); }

.empty-state {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 50px 20px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  color: var(--grey);
  margin: 30px 0;
}

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .site-form { padding: 26px; }
  .form-actions { flex-direction: column; }
}
```

```html
{% extends "admin/base_site.html" %}
{% load static %}

{# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   WHERE THIS GOES:
   Create a project-level templates directory (sibling to manage.py is
   fine), e.g.  C:\...\callsoso\templates\admin\base_site.html
   and save this file there.

   Then in settings.py, make sure that folder is in TEMPLATES[0]['DIRS']:

       TEMPLATES = [
           {
               ...
               'DIRS': [BASE_DIR / 'templates'],   # <-- add/confirm this
               'APP_DIRS': True,
               ...
           },
       ]

   This override MUST live in a DIRS-listed folder (not just any app's
   templates/ folder) to reliably win over Django's own admin templates,
   regardless of INSTALLED_APPS ordering.
--------------------------------------------------------------------- #}

{% block title %}{{ title }} — Call Soso Admin{% endblock %}

{% block branding %}
<h1 id="site-name">
    <a href="{% url 'admin:index' %}">Call Soso <span class="admin-suffix">Admin</span></a>
</h1>
{% endblock %}

{% block nav-global %}{% endblock %}

{% block extrastyle %}
{{ block.super }}
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:wght@500;600&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'admin/css/call_soso_admin.css' %}">
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\admin.py`

```python
from django.contrib import admin
from django.utils.html import format_html
from .models import (
    Article,
    Resource,
    Category,
    Collaboration,
    Contribution,
    FoundersList,
    MagazineIssue,
    PopularArticle
)


# ===========================
# CATEGORY
# ===========================
@admin.register(Category)
class CategoryAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "slug")
    prepopulated_fields = {"slug": ("name",)}
    search_fields = ("name",)
    ordering = ("name",)

# ===========================
# MAGAZINE ISSUE
# ===========================
@admin.register(MagazineIssue)
class MagazineIssueAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("title", "cover_thumb", "published_date", "is_featured", "is_published")
    list_filter = ("is_featured", "is_published", "published_date", "categories")
    search_fields = ("title", "description")
    filter_horizontal = ("categories",)
    prepopulated_fields = {"slug": ("title",)}
    date_hierarchy = "published_date"
    ordering = ("-published_date",)
    readonly_fields = ("cover_preview",)

    fieldsets = (
        ("Core", {
            "fields": ("title", "slug", "description", "categories"),
        }),
        ("Media", {
            "fields": ("cover_image", "cover_image_url", "cover_preview", "video_preview_url"),
            "description": "Upload a cover image or paste external URL. Optionally add a video preview.",
        }),
        ("Publishing", {
            "fields": ("published_date", "is_featured", "is_published"),
        }),
    )

    def cover_thumb(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="height:40px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "—"
    cover_thumb.short_description = "Cover"

    def cover_preview(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="max-height:220px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "No image yet"
    cover_preview.short_description = "Preview"

# ===========================
# POPULAR ARTICLES
# ===========================
@admin.register(PopularArticle)
class PopularArticleAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("title", "date")
    search_fields = ("title",)
    ordering = ("-date",)


# ===========================
# ARTICLE / NEWS
# ===========================
@admin.register(Article)
class ArticleAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "title",
        "image_thumb",
        "published_date",
        "is_published",
        "is_featured",
    )
    list_filter = (
        "is_published",
        "is_featured",
        "published_date",
        "categories",
    )
    search_fields = (
        "title",
        "body",
        "excerpt",
        "summary",
    )
    prepopulated_fields = {"slug": ("title",)}
    filter_horizontal = ("categories",)
    date_hierarchy = "published_date"
    ordering = ("-published_date",)
    readonly_fields = ("image_preview",)

    fieldsets = (
        ("Content", {
            "fields": ("title", "slug", "excerpt", "summary", "body"),
        }),
        ("Media", {
            "fields": ("image", "image_url", "image_preview"),
            "description": "Upload an image OR paste an external image URL (Pinterest, Unsplash, etc.)",
        }),
        ("Metadata", {
            "fields": (
                "author",
                "categories",
                "published_date",
                "is_featured",
                "is_published",
            ),
        }),
    )

    def image_thumb(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="height:40px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "—"
    image_thumb.short_description = "Image"

    def image_preview(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="max-height:220px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "No image yet"
    image_preview.short_description = "Preview"


# ===========================
# RESOURCE (Knowledge Center)
# ===========================
@admin.register(Resource)
class ResourceAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "title",
        "image_thumb",
        "resource_type",
        "published",
        "is_featured",
        "created_at",
    )
    list_filter = (
        "resource_type",
        "published",
        "is_featured",
        "categories",
    )
    search_fields = (
        "title",
        "description",
    )
    filter_horizontal = ("categories",)
    ordering = ("-created_at",)
    readonly_fields = ("image_preview",)

    fieldsets = (
        ("Core", {
            "fields": (
                "title",
                "description",
                "resource_type",
                "categories",
            ),
        }),
        ("Media", {
            "fields": ("image", "image_url", "image_preview"),
            "description": "Upload an image OR paste an external image URL (Pinterest, YouTube thumbnails, etc.)",
        }),
        ("Linking", {
            "fields": ("link",),
            "description": "Internal or external link (article, webinar page, YouTube, Medium, etc.)",
        }),
        ("Publishing", {
            "fields": (
                "published",
                "is_featured",
            ),
        }),
    )

    def image_thumb(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="height:40px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "—"
    image_thumb.short_description = "Image"

    def image_preview(self, obj):
        if obj.display_image:
            return format_html('<img src="{}" style="max-height:220px;width:auto;">', obj.display_image)
        return "No image yet"
    image_preview.short_description = "Preview"


# ===========================
# COLLABORATION
# ===========================
@admin.register(Collaboration)
class CollaborationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("name", "organisation", "growth_stage", "is_active", "planted_date")
    list_filter = ("growth_stage", "is_active")
    search_fields = ("name", "organisation", "description")
    ordering = ("-planted_date",)


# ===========================
# CONTRIBUTION
# ===========================
@admin.register(Contribution)
class ContributionAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("contributor_name", "source", "amount", "date")
    list_filter = ("source", "date")
    search_fields = ("contributor_name", "email", "message")
    ordering = ("-date",)


# ===========================
# FOUNDERS LIST
# ===========================
@admin.register(FoundersList)
class FoundersListAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("email", "joined")
    search_fields = ("email",)
    ordering = ("-joined",)
```

# FILE: `website\management\commands\create_admin.py`

```python
import os

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Create a Django superuser if one does not already exist"

    def handle(self, *args, **kwargs):

        User = get_user_model()

        username = os.environ.get("DJANGO_SUPERUSER_USERNAME")
        email = os.environ.get("DJANGO_SUPERUSER_EMAIL")
        password = os.environ.get("DJANGO_SUPERUSER_PASSWORD")

        if not username or not password:
            self.stdout.write(
                self.style.WARNING(
                    "Admin credentials are not configured. "
                    "Skipping superuser creation."
                )
            )
            return

        if User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            self.stdout.write(
                self.style.SUCCESS(
                    f"Superuser '{username}' already exists."
                )
            )
            return

        User.objects.create_superuser(
            username=username,
            email=email or "",
            password=password,
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Superuser '{username}' created successfully."
            )
        )
```

```python
                ('link', models.URLField(blank=True, help_text='Internal or external link (Pinterest, YouTube, Medium, etc.)')),
                ('is_featured', models.BooleanField(default=False)),
                ('published', models.BooleanField(default=True)),
                ('created_at', models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)),
                ('categories', models.ManyToManyField(blank=True, to='website.category')),
            ],
            options={
                'ordering': ['-created_at'],
            },
        ),
        migrations.AddField(
            model_name='article',
            name='categories',
            field=models.ManyToManyField(blank=True, to='website.category'),
        ),
    ]
```

# FILE: `website\migrations\0008_seed_default_categories.py`

```python
from django.db import migrations
from django.utils.text import slugify


DEFAULT_CATEGORIES = [
    "Circular Economy",
    "Corporate Surplus",
    "Upcycling & Makers",
    "Community Initiatives",
    "Sustainability & Climate",
    "Policy & Legislation",
    "Training & Courses",
    "Resource Management",
    "Best Practices",
    "Events & Webinars",
]


def seed_categories(apps, schema_editor):
    Category = apps.get_model("website", "Category")

    for name in DEFAULT_CATEGORIES:
        slug = slugify(name)

        Category.objects.get_or_create(
            slug=slug,
            defaults={
                "name": name,
            },
        )


def remove_seeded_categories(apps, schema_editor):
    Category = apps.get_model("website", "Category")

    Category.objects.filter(
        slug__in=[slugify(name) for name in DEFAULT_CATEGORIES]
    ).delete()


class Migration(migrations.Migration):

    dependencies = [
        ("website", "0007_alter_category_options"),
    ]

    operations = [
        migrations.RunPython(seed_categories, remove_seeded_categories),
    ]
```

```python
from django.db import migrations
from django.utils.text import slugify

DEFAULT_CATEGORIES = [
    "Circular Economy",
    "Corporate Surplus",
    "Upcycling & Makers",
    "Community Initiatives",
    "Sustainability & Climate",
    "Policy & Legislation",
    "Training & Courses",
    "Resource Management",
    "Best Practices",
    "Events & Webinars",
]


def update_categories(apps, schema_editor):
    Category = apps.get_model("website", "Category")

    # Remove the old defaults
    Category.objects.filter(
        slug__in=[
            "case-studies",
            "climate-impact",
            "community-culture",
            "design-upcycling",
            "education-learning",
            "materials-waste",
            "policy-systems",
        ]
    ).delete()

    # Ensure the new defaults exist
    for name in DEFAULT_CATEGORIES:
        slug = slugify(name)

        Category.objects.get_or_create(
            slug=slug,
            defaults={
                "name": name,
            },
        )


class Migration(migrations.Migration):

    dependencies = [
        ("website", "0008_seed_default_categories"),
    ]

    operations = [
        migrations.RunPython(update_categories),
    ]
```

# FILE: `website\models.py`

```python
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.urls import reverse
from django.utils.text import slugify
from django.utils import timezone
from django.templatetags.static import static

# ===========================
# CATEGORY (shared)
# ===========================
class Category(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    slug = models.SlugField(unique=True, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["name"]
        verbose_name = "Category"
        verbose_name_plural = "Categories"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            self.slug = slugify(self.name)
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

# ===========================
# MAGAZINE ISSUE
# ===========================
class MagazineIssue(models.Model):
    title = models.CharField(max_length=250)
    slug = models.SlugField(unique=True, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)
    cover_image = models.ImageField(upload_to="magazine/covers/", blank=True, null=True)
    cover_image_url = models.URLField(blank=True, null=True)
    video_preview_url = models.URLField(blank=True, null=True)
    published_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)
    categories = models.ManyToManyField(Category, blank=True)
    is_featured = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_published = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-published_date", "-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            base_slug = slugify(self.title)[:250]
            slug = base_slug
            i = 1
            while MagazineIssue.objects.filter(slug=slug).exclude(pk=self.pk).exists():
                slug = f"{base_slug}-{i}"
                i += 1
            self.slug = slug
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def get_absolute_url(self):
        try:
            return reverse("magazine_detail", kwargs={"slug": self.slug})
        except Exception:
            return "#"

    @property
    def display_image(self):
        if self.cover_image:
            return self.cover_image.url
        if self.cover_image_url:
            return self.cover_image_url
        return static("images/placeholder.jpg")

# ===========================
# POPULAR ARTICLES (Sidebar)
# ===========================
class PopularArticle(models.Model):
    title = models.CharField(max_length=250)
    url = models.URLField()
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to="articles/popular/", blank=True, null=True)
    image_url = models.URLField(blank=True, null=True)
    date = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-date"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    @property
    def display_image(self):
        if self.image:
            return self.image.url
        if self.image_url:
            return self.image_url
        return static("images/placeholder.jpg")



# ===========================
# COLLABORATION
# ===========================
class Collaboration(models.Model):
    GROWTH_STAGE_CHOICES = [
        (1, "Seed"),
        (2, "Sprout"),
        (3, "Growth"),
        (4, "Bloom"),
        (5, "Harvest"),
    ]

    name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField()
    organisation = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, null=True)
    contact_email = models.EmailField(blank=True, null=True)
    planted_date = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    growth_stage = models.IntegerField(choices=GROWTH_STAGE_CHOICES, default=1)
    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.name} (Stage {self.growth_stage})"


# ===========================
# CONTRIBUTION
# ===========================
class Contribution(models.Model):
    SOURCE_CHOICES = [
        ("donation", "Donation"),
        ("sponsorship", "Sponsorship"),
        ("grant", "Grant"),
        ("material", "Material Support"),
        ("other", "Other"),
    ]

    user = models.ForeignKey(User, on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True, blank=True)
    contributor_name = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True, null=True)
    email = models.EmailField(blank=True, null=True)
    amount = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2, null=True, blank=True)
    source = models.CharField(max_length=50, choices=SOURCE_CHOICES)
    message = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)
    date = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        name = self.contributor_name or (self.user.username if self.user else "Anonymous")
        return f"{name} – {self.source}"


# ===========================
# FOUNDERS LIST
# ===========================
class FoundersList(models.Model):
    email = models.EmailField(unique=True)
    joined = models.DateField(auto_now_add=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return self.email


# ===========================
# ARTICLE / NEWS (KEEPING)
# ===========================
class Article(models.Model):
    title = models.CharField(max_length=250)
    slug = models.SlugField(max_length=270, unique=True, blank=True)
    excerpt = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)
    summary = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)
    body = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)

    image = models.ImageField(upload_to="articles/images/", blank=True, null=True)
    image_url = models.URLField(blank=True, null=True)

    published_date = models.DateField(default=timezone.now)
    author = models.ForeignKey(
        User,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="articles",
    )

    categories = models.ManyToManyField(Category, blank=True)

    is_featured = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_published = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-published_date", "-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if not self.slug:
            base_slug = slugify(self.title)[:250]
            slug = base_slug
            i = 1
            while Article.objects.filter(slug=slug).exclude(pk=self.pk).exists():
                slug = f"{base_slug}-{i}"
                i += 1
            self.slug = slug
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def get_absolute_url(self):
        try:
            return reverse("article_detail", kwargs={"slug": self.slug})
        except Exception:
            return "#"

    @property
    def display_image(self):
        if self.image:
            return self.image.url
        if self.image_url:
            return self.image_url
        return static("images/placeholder.jpg")


# ===========================
# RESOURCE (KNOWLEDGE CENTER)
# ===========================
class Resource(models.Model):
    RESOURCE_TYPES = (
        ("highlight", "Highlight"),
        ("case_study", "Case Study"),
        ("webinar", "Webinar"),
        ("learning", "Learning"),
        ("other", "Other / Future"),
    )

    title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField(blank=True)

    resource_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=RESOURCE_TYPES,
    )

    categories = models.ManyToManyField(Category, blank=True)

    image = models.ImageField(
        upload_to="resources/images/",
        blank=True,
        null=True,
    )
    image_url = models.URLField(blank=True)

    link = models.URLField(
        blank=True,
        help_text="Internal or external link (Pinterest, YouTube, Medium, etc.)",
    )

    is_featured = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    published = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.title

    @property
    def display_image(self):
        if self.image:
            return self.image.url
        if self.image_url:
            return self.image_url
        return static("images/placeholder.jpg")
```

# FILE: `website\static\admin\css\call_soso_admin.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — DJANGO ADMIN THEME
   Save at: website/static/admin/css/call_soso_admin.css
   (loaded by templates/admin/base_site.html)
========================================================= */

/* Django's admin (3.2+) defines these as CSS custom properties in its
   own base.css — overriding them here reskins nearly everything without
   fighting individual selectors. */
   :root {
    --primary: #0a0a0a;
    --primary-fg: #ffffff;

    --secondary: #ff5a1f;
    --secondary-fg: #ffffff;

    --accent: #ff5a1f;

    --header-color: #ffffff;
    --header-branding-color: #ffffff;
    --header-bg: #0a0a0a;
    --header-link-color: #ffffff;

    --breadcrumbs-fg: #6b6b6b;
    --breadcrumbs-link-fg: #0a0a0a;
    --breadcrumbs-bg: #f5f4f1;

    --link-fg: #0a0a0a;
    --link-hover-color: #ff5a1f;
    --link-selected-fg: #ff5a1f;

    --button-bg: #0a0a0a;
    --button-fg: #ffffff;
    --button-hover-bg: #ff5a1f;
    --default-button-bg: #ff5a1f;
    --default-button-hover-bg: #0a0a0a;

    --object-tools-bg: #ff5a1f;
    --object-tools-hover-bg: #0a0a0a;

    --font-family-primary: 'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

body {
    font-family: var(--font-family-primary);
}

/* Branding — serif nameplate to match the front-end site */
#site-name a {
    font-family: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 1.4rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
}
#site-name .admin-suffix {
    font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
    font-weight: 400;
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    opacity: 0.6;
    margin-left: 6px;
}

/* Fallback concrete overrides for older Django versions without the
   CSS-variable theme (harmless no-ops on newer versions since the
   variables above already cover it). */
#header {
    background: #0a0a0a;
    color: #ffffff;
}
#header a:link, #header a:visited {
    color: #ffffff;
}
div.breadcrumbs {
    background: #f5f4f1;
    color: #6b6b6b;
}
.module h2, .module caption, .inline-group h2 {
    background: #0a0a0a;
    color: #ffffff;
}
a.button, button, input[type=submit], input[type=button], .submit-row input {
    background: #0a0a0a;
    color: #ffffff;
}
a.button:hover, button:hover, input[type=submit]:hover, input[type=button]:hover {
    background: #ff5a1f;
}
.default, input[type=submit].default, .submit-row input.default {
    background: #ff5a1f;
}
.default:hover {
    background: #0a0a0a;
}

/* Image thumbnails in change-list (used by the ModelAdmin list_display
   preview methods below) */
.field-cover_thumb img,
.field-image_thumb img {
    border: 1px solid #e5e3de;
    display: block;
}

/* =========================================================
   DASHBOARD (index page) — the app-list boxes become hairline
   tiles instead of Django's default blue module boxes.
========================================================= */
#content-main .app-list,
#content-main .module {
    background: #ffffff;
    border: 1px solid #e5e3de;
    box-shadow: none;
    border-radius: 0;
    margin-bottom: 24px;
}
#content-main .module caption,
#content-main .module caption a {
    background: #ffffff;
    color: #0a0a0a;
    font-family: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
    text-transform: none;
    border-bottom: 1px solid #e5e3de;
    padding: 14px 16px;
}
#content-main table {
    border: none;
}
#content-main table th,
#content-main table td {
    border-bottom: 1px solid #f0efeb;
    padding: 10px 16px;
}
#content-main table th a {
    color: #0a0a0a;
    font-weight: 500;
}
#content-main table th a:hover {
    color: #ff5a1f;
}
#content-main .addlink, #content-main .changelink {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: #6b6b6b;
}
#content-main .addlink:hover, #content-main .changelink:hover {
    color: #ff5a1f;
}

/* Recent actions sidebar panel */
#content-related .module {
    background: #ffffff;
    border: 1px solid #e5e3de;
    border-radius: 0;
}
#content-related h2, #content-related h3 {
    font-family: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #0a0a0a;
}
#recent-actions-module a {
    color: #0a0a0a;
}
#recent-actions-module a:hover {
    color: #ff5a1f;
}

/* =========================================================
   LOGIN PAGE
========================================================= */
body.login {
    background: #0a0a0a;
}
body.login #container {
    background: #ffffff;
    border-radius: 0;
    box-shadow: 0 30px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}
body.login .submit-row input {
    background: #0a0a0a;
}
body.login .submit-row input:hover {
    background: #ff5a1f;
}
```
