# FILE: `website\static\css\about.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — ABOUT
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem); text-align: center; margin: 0 0 20px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); line-height: 1.65; }
.lede.center { text-align: center; max-width: 700px; margin: 0 auto 16px; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 13px 28px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light { border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* HERO */
.about-hero {
  position: relative;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 110px 20px;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
  color: var(--white);
}
.about-hero::before { content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(10,10,10,.78), rgba(10,10,10,.55)); }
.about-hero-inner { position: relative; z-index: 1; max-width: 720px; margin: 0 auto; }
.about-hero .section-title { color: var(--white); }
.about-hero .lede { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); }
.about-hero .hero-microcopy { font-family: var(--font-display); font-style: italic; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); margin-top: 14px; }

/* LAYOUT */
.about-container { max-width: 1000px; margin: 0 auto; }
.about-section { padding: 90px 40px; border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); }
.about-container .about-section:first-child { border-top: none; }

.about-section.dark-block { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); border-top: none; }
.dark-block .lede { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.dark-block h4 { color: var(--white); }
.dark-block p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }

/* HOW IT WORKS CARDS */
.about-cards {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr));
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-top: 40px;
}
.about-card { background: var(--white); padding: 34px; }
.card-num { display: block; font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.4rem; color: var(--orange); margin-bottom: 10px; }
.about-card h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.3rem; margin: 0 0 10px; }
.about-card p { color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.92rem; line-height: 1.55; margin: 0; }

/* IMPACT */
.impact-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-top: 40px;
}
.impact-item { background: var(--white); padding: 34px; text-align: center; }
.impact-number { display: block; font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 2.4rem; color: var(--orange); margin-bottom: 8px; }
.impact-item p { color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0; }

/* FOUNDERS */
.founders-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 40px;
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 40px auto 0;
  text-align: left;
}
.founders-grid h4 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.2rem; margin: 0 0 8px; }

.founders-form {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 14px;
  margin: 40px auto 0;
}
.founders-form input[type="email"] {
  padding: 13px 18px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35);
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--white);
  outline: none;
  min-width: 300px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}
.founders-form input[type="email"]::placeholder { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
.founders-form input[type="email"]:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

/* CTA */
.about-cta { padding: 90px 40px; text-align: center; background: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.about-cta .section-title { color: var(--white); }
.about-cta .lede { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.9); }
.about-cta .btn-outline.light:hover { background: var(--black); border-color: var(--black); }

/* RESPONSIVE */
@media (max-width: 700px) {
  .about-section { padding: 60px 24px; }
  .founders-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 26px; }
}
```

# FILE: `website\static\css\article_detail.css`

```css
/* =========================
   ARTICLE DETAIL PAGE
========================= */

/* Hero / Header */
.article-hero {
    background: var(--cream-light);
    padding: 2rem 1.5rem;
    text-align: center;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-soft);
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.article-hero h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
    color: var(--green-dark);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.article-hero .date {
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    color: var(--green-muted);
    margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.article-hero .categories {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: var(--green-muted);
}

/* Feature Image */
.article-image {
    text-align: center;
    margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.article-image img {
    max-width: 100%;
    height: auto;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-soft);
}

/* Article Body */
.article-body {
    max-width: 800px;
    margin: 0 auto 3rem auto;
    padding: 0 1rem;
    line-height: 1.65;
    color: var(--green-dark);
}

.article-body .excerpt {
    font-weight: 600;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
    color: var(--green-dark);
}

.article-body .summary {
    margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
    font-size: 1rem;
}

.article-body .body-content {
    font-size: 1rem;
}

/* Related Articles */
.related-articles {
    max-width: 800px;
    margin: 0 auto 3rem auto;
    padding: 1rem;
    background: var(--cream-light);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-soft);
}

.related-articles h3 {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
    color: var(--green-dark);
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.related-articles ul {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin: 0;
}

.related-articles li {
    padding: 0.5rem 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--green-muted-light);
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
}

.related-articles li:last-child {
    border-bottom: none;
}

.related-articles a {
    color: var(--earth-brown);
    text-decoration: none;
    font-weight: 500;
    transition: color 0.2s ease;
}

.related-articles a:hover {
    color: var(--clay-accent);
}

.related-articles .date {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--green-muted);
}

/* Responsive adjustments */
@media screen and (max-width: 768px) {
    .article-body,
    .related-articles {
        padding: 0 1rem;
    }

    .article-hero h1 {
        font-size: 1.75rem;
    }
}
```

```css
:root{
    --black:#0a0a0a;
    --white:#ffffff;
    --off-white:#f7f6f3;
    --orange:#ff5a1f;
    --grey:#6b6b6b;
    --grey-line:rgba(10,10,10,.08);

    --font-display:'Cormorant Garamond',serif;
    --font-sans:'Inter',sans-serif;
}

*{
    box-sizing:border-box;
}

.categories-page{
    max-width:1200px;
    margin:auto;
    padding:90px 40px 100px;
    font-family:var(--font-sans);
    color:var(--black);
}

.categories-header{
    text-align:center;
    margin-bottom:70px;
}

.eyebrow{
    text-transform:uppercase;
    letter-spacing:.24em;
    color:var(--grey);
    font-size:.72rem;
    margin-bottom:12px;
}

.section-title{
    font-family:var(--font-display);
    font-size:clamp(2.2rem,4vw,3rem);
    font-weight:500;
    margin:0;
}

.lede{
    max-width:650px;
    margin:18px auto 0;
    color:var(--grey);
    line-height:1.7;
}

.categories-grid{

    display:grid;

    grid-template-columns:
        repeat(auto-fit,minmax(290px,1fr));

    gap:24px;
}

.category-tile{

    background:white;

    border:1px solid var(--grey-line);

    padding:34px;

    transition:.25s;
}

.category-tile:hover{

    transform:translateY(-6px);

    border-color:rgba(255,90,31,.25);

    box-shadow:0 18px 45px rgba(0,0,0,.08);

}

.category-header{

    display:flex;

    justify-content:space-between;

    align-items:flex-start;

    gap:20px;

    margin-bottom:26px;

}

.category-heading{

    display:flex;

    flex-direction:column;

    gap:8px;

    flex:1;

}

.category-heading h2{

    margin:0;

    font-family:var(--font-display);

    font-weight:500;

    font-size:1.55rem;

    line-height:1.2;

}

.category-heading h2 a{

    color:var(--black);

    text-decoration:none;

    transition:.25s;

}

.category-heading h2 a:hover{

    color:var(--orange);

}

.browse-category{

    display:inline-flex;

    align-items:center;

    gap:8px;

    width:fit-content;

    text-decoration:none;

    font-size:.76rem;

    letter-spacing:.08em;

    text-transform:uppercase;

    color:var(--grey);

    transition:.25s;

}

.browse-category:hover{

    color:var(--orange);

}

.browse-category span{

    transition:.25s;

}

.browse-category:hover span{

    transform:translateX(4px);

}

.category-total{

    white-space:nowrap;

    background:#f4f4f4;

    padding:7px 12px;

    border-radius:999px;

    font-size:.72rem;

    color:var(--grey);

}

.tile-links{

    display:flex;

    flex-direction:column;

    gap:14px;

}

.tile-link{

    display:flex;

    justify-content:space-between;

    align-items:center;

    text-decoration:none;

    color:var(--black);

    padding:4px 0 12px;

    border-bottom:1px solid var(--grey-line);

    transition:.25s;

}

.tile-link:hover{

    color:var(--orange);

    padding-left:6px;

}

.tile-link.muted{

    opacity:.45;

}

.tile-count{

    color:var(--orange);

    font-family:var(--font-display);

    font-size:1.15rem;

    font-weight:600;

}

.empty-state{

    text-align:center;

    border:1px solid var(--grey-line);

    padding:70px 40px;

}

.empty-sub{

    color:var(--grey);

}

@media(max-width:700px){

.categories-page{

padding:70px 20px;

}

.category-header{

flex-direction:column;

gap:12px;

}

}
```

# FILE: `website\static\css\contact.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — CONTACT
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.contact-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 90px 40px 100px; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 3rem); text-align: center; margin: 0 0 16px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 560px; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 13px 28px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light { border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white); }

/* HERO */
.contact-header { margin-bottom: 70px; }
.hero-inner { max-width: 640px; margin: 0 auto; }

/* LAYOUT */
.contact-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr;
  gap: 60px;
}

/* FORM */
.contact-form-section { border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding: 44px; }
.contact-form { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 24px; }
.form-group { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.form-group label {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.form-group .required { color: var(--orange); }
.form-group input,
.form-group select,
.form-group textarea {
  padding: 13px 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  color: var(--black);
  outline: none;
}
.form-group input:focus,
.form-group select:focus,
.form-group textarea:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }
textarea { resize: vertical; min-height: 140px; }
.contact-form .btn-outline { align-self: flex-start; }

/* SIDEBAR */
.contact-aside { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 30px; }
.aside-block, .aside-cta { padding: 30px; border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); }
.aside-block h5, .aside-cta h5 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  margin: 0 0 16px;
}
.aside-block ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
.aside-block li { margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--grey); }
.aside-block a { color: var(--black); font-weight: 500; }
.aside-block a:hover { color: var(--orange); }

.aside-cta { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); border-color: var(--black); }
.aside-cta p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); margin: 0 0 18px; font-size: 0.92rem; }

/* MICROCOPY */
.contact-microcopy { margin-top: 60px; text-align: center; color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.92rem; }
.contact-microcopy p { margin: 6px 0; }
.contact-microcopy a { color: var(--orange); }

/* RESPONSIVE */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .contact-layout { grid-template-columns: 1fr; gap: 30px; }
  .contact-form-section { padding: 28px; }
}
```

# FILE: `website\static\css\home.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — HOME
   Shares the token system defined in base.html.
   Redeclared here so home.css can stand alone if needed.
========================================================= */
:root {
    --black:        #0a0a0a;
    --white:        #ffffff;
    --off-white:    #f5f4f1;
    --orange:       #ff5a1f;
    --grey:         #6b6b6b;
    --grey-line:    rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

    --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;

    /* Loop duotones — kept distinct from the orange accent on purpose:
       these are the two material worlds the studio works in. */
    --material-a: #1c2b45;
    --material-b: #3f5c8a;
    --edible-a:   #1f3a2a;
    --edible-b:   #4f7a52;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

body {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    color: var(--black);
    line-height: 1.6;
    background: var(--white);
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
}

a { text-decoration: none; color: inherit; }

.eyebrow {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.22em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--grey);
    display: block;
    margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 3rem);
    text-align: center;
    margin: 0 0 40px;
}

.lede {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 1.05rem;
    color: var(--grey);
    max-width: 720px;
}
.lede.center { margin: 0 auto 20px; text-align: center; }

.btn-outline {
    display: inline-block;
    border: 1px solid var(--black);
    color: var(--black);
    background: transparent;
    padding: 13px 30px;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.16em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    cursor: pointer;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light { border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
   HERO — split material/edible duotone backdrop, slow drift
========================================================= */
.hero-main {
    position: relative;
    height: 100vh;
    min-height: 640px;
    overflow: hidden;
    color: var(--white);
}

.hero-bg {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    display: flex;
    z-index: 0;
}
.hero-bg-half {
    flex: 1;
    position: relative;
    background-size: 130% 130%;
    background-position: center;
    background-repeat: no-repeat;
    animation: hero-drift 24s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
}
.hero-bg-half.material { background-color: var(--material-a); }
.hero-bg-half.edible   { background-color: var(--edible-a); animation-delay: -12s; }

.hero-bg-half::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}
.hero-bg-half.material::before { background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(28,43,69,.55), rgba(63,92,138,.35)); }
.hero-bg-half.edible::before   { background: linear-gradient(200deg, rgba(31,58,42,.55), rgba(79,122,82,.35)); }
/* Photos live at static/images/hero-material.jpg and hero-edible.jpg — set inline
   in home.html so {% static %} resolves. Until a file exists, the background-color
   above shows instead of a broken image. */

@keyframes hero-drift {
    from { background-size: 130% 130%; background-position: 45% 45%; }
    to   { background-size: 145% 145%; background-position: 55% 55%; }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .hero-bg-half { animation: none; }
}

.hero-overlay {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
    height: 100%;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 40px;
}

.corner-note {
    position: absolute;
    top: 40px;
    max-width: 280px;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85);
}
.corner-note.top-left  { left: 40px; text-align: left; }
.corner-note.top-right { right: 40px; text-align: right; }

.hero-center .wordmark {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 600;
    font-size: clamp(3.5rem, 9vw, 7rem);
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    margin: 0;
    color: var(--white);
}
.hero-center .hero-italic {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-style: italic;
    font-size: 1.4rem;
    color: var(--off-white);
    margin: 10px 0 30px;
}
.hero-center .hero-line {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75);
}

.scroll-cue {
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 40px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.2em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
}
.scroll-cue .chevron { animation: bounce 2s infinite; font-size: 1rem; }
@keyframes bounce {
    0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); }
    50% { transform: translateY(6px); }
}

/* =========================================================
   INTRO STATEMENT
========================================================= */
.intro-statement {
    text-align: center;
    padding: 110px 20px;
    max-width: 780px;
    margin: 0 auto;
}
.intro-statement h2 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.4vw, 2.6rem);
    margin: 0 0 18px;
}

/* =========================================================
   MATERIAL / EDIBLE LOOP SPLIT
========================================================= */
.loops-split {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    min-height: 70vh;
}
.loop-panel {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 70px;
    color: var(--white);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
}
.loop-panel.material { background-color: var(--material-a); }
.loop-panel.edible   { background-color: var(--edible-a); }
.loop-panel::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
}
.loop-panel.material::before { background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(28,43,69,.75), rgba(63,92,138,.5)); }
.loop-panel.edible::before   { background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(31,58,42,.75), rgba(79,122,82,.5)); }
.loop-panel > * { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
/* Photos live at static/images/loop-material.jpg and loop-edible.jpg, set inline
   in home.html. background-color above is the fallback until those files exist. */
.loop-panel .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.loop-panel h2 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: clamp(1.6rem, 2.6vw, 2.2rem);
    max-width: 420px;
    margin: 0 0 30px;
}
.loop-panel .panel-link {
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.6);
    padding-bottom: 4px;
    width: fit-content;
}
.loop-panel .arrow { margin-left: 4px; }
.loop-panel:hover .panel-link { border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
   DIRECTORY / SITE INDEX
========================================================= */
.directory-index {
    padding: 0 40px 40px;
    max-width: 1280px;
    margin: 0 auto;
}
.index-row {
    display: grid;
    gap: 1px;
    background: var(--grey-line);
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    margin-top: -1px;
}
.index-row.featured-row { grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr; }
.index-row.three-up     { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); }

.index-card {
    position: relative;
    background: var(--white);
    padding: 44px 40px;
    min-height: 260px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: flex-end;
}
.index-card .index-num {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    color: var(--grey);
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    margin-bottom: auto;
}
.index-card h3, .index-card h2 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: 1.7rem;
    margin: 0 0 10px;
}
.index-card p { color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0 0 14px; }
.index-card:hover { background: var(--off-white); }

.index-card.photo {
    background-color: var(--black);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    color: var(--white);
}
.index-card.photo::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(10,10,10,.78), rgba(10,10,10,.5));
}
.index-card.photo > * { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
/* Photos live at static/images/index-about.jpg, index-creative-systems.jpg,
   index-narrative.jpg, index-living-system.jpg — set inline in home.html.
   Kept to a neutral black scrim (rather than the loop colors) so these four
   read as the same "index" family, while Material/Edible/Featured/Garden
   keep their distinct thematic hues. background-color above is the fallback
   until the files exist. */
.index-card.photo .index-num { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.65); }
.index-card.photo h3 { color: var(--white); }
.index-card.photo p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.index-card.photo .panel-link.small { color: var(--white); border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); }
.index-card.photo:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }
.index-card.photo:hover::before { background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(255,90,31,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.6)); }

.index-card.featured {
    background-color: var(--orange);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    color: var(--white);
    justify-content: center;
}
.index-card.featured::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(255,90,31,.85), rgba(10,10,10,.45));
}
.index-card.featured > * { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
/* Photo lives at static/images/directory-featured.jpg, set inline in home.html.
   background-color above is the fallback until that file exists. */
.index-card.featured .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); }
.index-card.featured h2 { font-size: 2.2rem; }
.index-card.featured p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); }
.index-card.featured .panel-link {
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.6);
    padding-bottom: 4px;
    width: fit-content;
}
.index-card.featured:hover::before { background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(10,10,10,.75), rgba(10,10,10,.6)); }

.index-card.wide {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    background-color: var(--edible-a);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    color: var(--white);
    padding: 44px 50px;
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    margin-top: -1px;
}
.index-card.wide::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(31,58,42,.8), rgba(79,122,82,.5));
}
.index-card.wide > * { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
/* Photo lives at static/images/garden.jpg, set inline in home.html.
   background-color above is the fallback until that file exists. */
.index-card.wide .garden-main { max-width: 420px; }
.index-card.wide .index-num { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); margin: 0 0 10px; display: block; }
.index-card.wide h3 { margin: 0 0 8px; }
.index-card.wide p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); margin: 0; }
.index-card.wide .garden-microcopy {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 6px;
    text-align: right;
    margin: 0 30px;
}
.index-card.wide .garden-microcopy p {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-style: italic;
    font-size: 0.95rem;
    color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75);
}
.index-card.wide .arrow { font-size: 1.4rem; flex-shrink: 0; }

@media (max-width: 900px) {
    .loops-split { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .index-row.featured-row,
    .index-row.three-up { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .index-card.wide { flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 18px; }
    .index-card.wide .garden-microcopy { text-align: left; margin: 0; }
}

/* =========================================================
   LATEST STORIES — same photo + tint + white-text pattern as
   the loop panels and index cards, using the article's own image.
========================================================= */
.latest-articles { padding: 100px 40px; max-width: 1280px; margin: 0 auto; }

.latest-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
    gap: 1px;
    background: var(--grey-line);
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

.story-card {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 340px;
    background-color: var(--black);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
}
.story-card::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.85));
}
.story-body { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 28px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; flex: 1; justify-content: flex-end; }
.story-meta { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }
.story-body h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.4rem; margin: 0; color: var(--white); }
.story-excerpt { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); line-height: 1.55; font-size: 0.92rem; }
.story-body .panel-link.small { color: var(--white); border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); margin-top: 4px; }
.story-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

.no-articles {
    text-align: center;
    padding: 60px 20px;
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    color: var(--grey);
}
.no-articles a { color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
   FEATURED RESOURCES — same treatment, using the resource's image.
========================================================= */
.featured-resources { padding: 0 40px 100px; max-width: 1280px; margin: 0 auto; }

.resource-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
    gap: 1px;
    background: var(--grey-line);
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

.resource-card {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 300px;
    background-color: var(--black);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
}
.resource-card::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.85));
}
.resource-body { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 28px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; flex: 1; justify-content: flex-end; }
.resource-body h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.3rem; margin: 0; color: var(--white); }
.resource-body p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); line-height: 1.55; font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0; }
.panel-link.small {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--white);
    width: fit-content;
    padding-bottom: 2px;
    color: var(--white);
    margin-top: 4px;
}
.resource-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
   ALTERNATING CONTENT SECTIONS
========================================================= */
.dark-block {
    background: var(--black);
    color: var(--white);
    padding: 100px 40px;
}
.dark-block .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); }
.dark-block .lede { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.dark-block h3 { color: var(--white); }
.dark-block p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }

.manifesto, .services, .support, .closing-cta {
    padding: 100px 40px;
    text-align: center;
}

.two-col {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 50px;
    max-width: 900px;
    margin: 40px auto 0;
    text-align: left;
}
.two-col h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: 1.4rem;
    margin: 0 0 10px;
}
.closing-cta .two-col .eyebrow { margin-bottom: 10px; }
.closing-cta .two-col p:not(.eyebrow) { color: var(--grey); margin: 0 0 20px; }
.two-col p { color: var(--grey); margin: 0; }

.founders-bios { max-width: 1000px; }

.founders-form {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 14px;
    margin: 50px auto 0;
}
.founders-form input[type="email"] {
    padding: 13px 18px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.35);
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--white);
    outline: none;
    min-width: 300px;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    font-size: 0.9rem;
}
.founders-form input[type="email"]::placeholder { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
.founders-form input[type="email"]:focus { border-color: var(--orange); }

.services-lists {
    max-width: 900px;
    text-align: left;
}
.services-lists ul { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
.services-lists li {
    padding: 14px 0;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    font-size: 0.95rem;
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
    .two-col, .services-lists { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .hero-bg { flex-direction: column; }
    .corner-note { position: static; max-width: none; margin-bottom: 14px; }
    .hero-overlay { padding-top: 60px; gap: 10px; }
}
```

# FILE: `website\static\css\impact_tracer.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — IMPACT TRACKER (THE GARDEN)
   Editorial high-contrast design, centered body/text,
   1px grid borders, and forest green duotone accents.
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:        #0a0a0a;
  --white:        #ffffff;
  --off-white:    #f5f4f1;
  --orange:       #ff5a1f;
  --grey:         #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line:    rgba(10, 10, 10, 0.1);
  
  /* Garden thematic duotone tokens */
  --forest-dark:  #1f3a2a;
  --forest-light: #4f7a52;

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

body {
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
}

/* =========================================================
 PAGE WRAPPER & CENTERED UTILITIES
========================================================= */
.tracker-page {
  max-width: 1280px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 60px 40px 120px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  text-align: center;
}

.eyebrow {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(2.4rem, 5vw, 3.8rem);
  line-height: 1.15;
  margin: 0 0 18px;
  color: var(--black);
  text-align: center;
}

.lede {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 1.05rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: var(--grey);
}
.lede.center {
  text-align: center;
  max-width: 640px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

/* Buttons & Interactive Elements */
.btn-outline {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 13px 30px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.25s ease;
  text-decoration: none;
}
.btn-outline:hover {
  background: var(--orange);
  border-color: var(--orange);
  color: var(--white);
}
.btn-outline.muted {
  border-color: var(--grey-line);
  color: var(--grey);
}
.btn-outline.muted:hover {
  border-color: var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
}

/* =========================================================
 HERO & CONTROLS
========================================================= */
.tracker-hero {
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 860px;
  margin-bottom: 70px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
}

.hero-microcopy {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 1.05rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin: 12px 0 36px;
}

.tracker-controls {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 14px;
  width: 100%;
}

.select-wrapper {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-block;
}

.tracker-controls select {
  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  padding: 13px 38px 13px 20px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  outline: none;
  cursor: pointer;
  border-radius: 0;
  transition: border-color 0.25s ease, color 0.25s ease;
}

.select-wrapper::after {
  content: "▾";
  position: absolute;
  right: 16px;
  top: 50%;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  font-size: 0.65rem;
  pointer-events: none;
  color: var(--grey);
}

.tracker-controls select:focus,
.tracker-controls select:hover {
  border-color: var(--orange);
  color: var(--orange);
}

/* =========================================================
 FOREST SUMMARY (GARDEN DUOTONE 1PX GRID)
========================================================= */
.forest-summary {
  width: 100%;
  background: var(--forest-dark);
  color: var(--white);
  border: 1px solid var(--forest-dark);
  margin-bottom: 80px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.summary-header {
  padding: 44px 40px 30px;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(31,58,42,0.9), rgba(79,122,82,0.7));
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}
.summary-header .eyebrow {
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.65);
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}
.summary-header h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 2.2rem;
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--white);
}

.summary-bars {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(5, 1fr);
  gap: 1px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  width: 100%;
}

.summary-bar {
  background: var(--forest-dark);
  padding: 32px 24px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
}

.summary-bar .bar-label {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75);
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.summary-bar .bar-track {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 120px;
  height: 4px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  margin: 0 auto 16px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.summary-bar .bar-fill {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  width: 0;
  background: var(--orange);
  transition: width 1.2s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}

.summary-bar .bar-count {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-style: normal;
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.8rem;
  color: var(--white);
  line-height: 1;
}

/* =========================================================
 FOREST GRID (CENTERED TEXT & 1PX BORDER GAP)
========================================================= */
.forest-grid {
  width: 100%;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 70px;
}

.seed-card {
  position: relative;
  background: var(--white);
  padding: 44px 34px;
  min-height: 320px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  text-align: center;
  transition: background 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
  border-top: 3px solid transparent;
}

.seed-card:hover {
  background: var(--off-white);
  border-top-color: var(--orange);
  transform: translateY(-4px);
  z-index: 2;
}

.card-top {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
}

.stage-num {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.68rem;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.18em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--orange);
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.seed-card h4 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  line-height: 1.25;
  margin: 0 0 14px;
  color: var(--black);
}

.seed-card p {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.88rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
}

.card-bottom {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  margin-top: 28px;
  padding-top: 20px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

.planted-date {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.stage-dots {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.stage-dots .dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  transition: background 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
}

.stage-dots .dot.filled {
  background: var(--orange);
  transform: scale(1.2);
}

/* Empty State / No Seeds */
.no-seeds {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  background: var(--white);
  padding: 80px 40px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
  gap: 20px;
}
.no-seeds p {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  max-width: 500px;
  margin: 0;
}

/* =========================================================
 QUICK ACTIONS
========================================================= */
.tracker-actions {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 16px;
  width: 100%;
}

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE BREAKPOINTS
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 1024px) {
  .summary-bars {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .tracker-page {
      padding: 40px 20px 80px;
  }
  
  .summary-bars {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
  }
  
  .summary-bar:last-child {
      grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }
  
  .forest-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .tracker-controls {
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: stretch;
  }
  
  .select-wrapper,
  .tracker-controls select,
  .tracker-controls .btn-outline {
      width: 100%;
      text-align: center;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 480px) {
  .summary-bars {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  .summary-bar:last-child {
      grid-column: auto;
  }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — INSIGHTS
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.eyebrow {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }

.section-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem);
  margin: 0 0 14px;
}

.hairline-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
.empty-state { color: var(--grey); }

.panel-link.small {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--white);
  width: fit-content;
  padding-bottom: 2px;
  color: var(--white);
  margin-top: 4px;
  display: inline-block;
}
.article-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 HERO — same photo + neutral black tint as other content pages
========================================================= */
.insights-hero {
  position: relative;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 100px 20px;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
  color: var(--white);
}
.insights-hero::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(10,10,10,.78), rgba(10,10,10,.55));
}
.insights-hero-inner { position: relative; z-index: 1; max-width: 700px; margin: 0 auto; }
.insights-hero .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.insights-hero .section-title { color: var(--white); }
.insights-hero .hero-microcopy {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-style: italic;
  color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75);
  margin-top: 10px;
}

/* =========================================================
 PAGE
========================================================= */
.insights-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1300px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 40px 100px; }

/* =========================================================
 TOOLBAR — filter pills, same visual language as Magazine/Knowledge
========================================================= */
.insights-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 44px 0 24px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 60px;
}
.filter-pills { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; }
.filter-pill {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 9px 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.03em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: border-color .2s ease, background .2s ease, color .2s ease;
}
.filter-pill:hover { border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--orange); }
.filter-pill.active { background: var(--black); border-color: var(--black); color: var(--white); }
.pill-count {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: var(--orange);
  opacity: 0.85;
}
.filter-pill.active .pill-count { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }

.browsing-label { font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--grey); }
.browsing-label span { color: var(--orange); font-weight: 600; }

/* =========================================================
 LAYOUT — main grid + slim Recently Published sidebar
========================================================= */
.insights-wrapper {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 280px;
  gap: 50px;
  align-items: start;
}

/* SIDEBAR */
.sidebar-section { margin-bottom: 46px; }
.insights-sidebar h3 {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin: 0 0 16px;
}
.popular-list li { padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 3px; }
.popular-list a { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1rem; color: var(--black); }
.popular-list a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
.popular-list .date { font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--grey); }

/* =========================================================
 MAIN — single flat editorial grid (no more per-category sections)
========================================================= */
.articles-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

/* Article cards — same photo + tint + white-text pattern as the rest of the site */
.article-card {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 320px;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
}
.article-card::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.85));
}
.article-body { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 26px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; flex: 1; justify-content: flex-end; }
.article-category { font-size: 0.68rem; letter-spacing: 0.1em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--orange); }
.article-date { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }
.article-body h4 { margin: 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.25rem; }
.article-body h4 a { color: var(--white); }
.article-card:hover .article-body h4 a { color: var(--orange); }
.excerpt { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); font-size: 0.9rem; line-height: 1.5; }

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 980px) {
  .insights-page { padding: 0 20px 80px; }
  .insights-wrapper { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .insights-sidebar { order: -1; }
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .insights-toolbar { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — KNOWLEDGE CENTER
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem); margin: 0 0 14px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 620px; }
.empty-state { color: var(--grey); }

.hairline-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }

.panel-link.small {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--white);
  width: fit-content;
  padding-bottom: 2px;
  color: var(--white);
  margin-top: 4px;
  display: inline-block;
}
.resource-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* HERO — same photo + neutral tint pattern as other content pages */
.knowledge-hero {
  position: relative;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 100px 20px;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
  color: var(--white);
}
.knowledge-hero::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(160deg, rgba(10,10,10,.78), rgba(10,10,10,.55));
}
.knowledge-hero-inner { position: relative; z-index: 1; max-width: 720px; margin: 0 auto; }
.knowledge-hero .section-title { color: var(--white); }
.knowledge-hero .hero-microcopy { font-family: var(--font-display); font-style: italic; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); margin-top: 14px; }

/* PAGE */
.knowledge-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1300px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 40px 100px; }

/* =========================================================
 TOOLBAR — filter pills, same visual language as Magazine
========================================================= */
.knowledge-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 44px 0 24px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 60px;
}
.filter-pills { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; }
.filter-pill {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 9px 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.03em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: border-color .2s ease, background .2s ease, color .2s ease;
}
.filter-pill:hover { border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--orange); }
.filter-pill.active { background: var(--black); border-color: var(--black); color: var(--white); }
.pill-count {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: var(--orange);
  opacity: 0.85;
}
.filter-pill.active .pill-count { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }

.browsing-label { font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--grey); }
.browsing-label span { color: var(--orange); font-weight: 600; }

/* =========================================================
 LAYOUT — main content + slim Recently Published sidebar
========================================================= */
.knowledge-wrapper {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 280px;
  gap: 50px;
  align-items: start;
}

/* SIDEBAR */
.sidebar-section { margin-bottom: 46px; }
.knowledge-sidebar h3 { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); margin: 0 0 16px; }

.popular-list li { padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 3px; }
.popular-list a, .popular-list span { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1rem; color: var(--black); }
.popular-list a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
.popular-list .date { font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--grey); }

/* MAIN */
.knowledge-main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 60px; }
.knowledge-section { border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding-top: 40px; }
.knowledge-main .knowledge-section:first-child { border-top: none; padding-top: 0; }

.resources-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

/* Same photo + tint + white-text card pattern used across the site */
.resource-card {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 300px;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
}
.resource-card::before {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.85));
}
.resource-body { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 26px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; flex: 1; justify-content: flex-end; }
.resource-badge {
  display: inline-block;
  width: fit-content;
  font-size: 0.62rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5);
  color: var(--white);
  padding: 3px 8px;
  margin-bottom: 6px;
}
.resource-date { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }
.resource-body h4 { margin: 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.25rem; color: var(--white); }
.resource-categories { font-size: 0.75rem; letter-spacing: 0.04em; color: var(--orange); }
.resource-body p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); font-size: 0.9rem; line-height: 1.5; margin: 0; }

/* RESPONSIVE */
@media (max-width: 980px) {
  .knowledge-page { padding: 0 20px 80px; }
  .knowledge-wrapper { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .knowledge-sidebar { order: -1; }
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .knowledge-toolbar { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — LOOPS
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.loops-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1200px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 90px 40px 110px; }

.eyebrow {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}

.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); line-height: 1.65; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 13px 28px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }

/* =========================================================
 HERO
========================================================= */
.loops-hero { margin-bottom: 100px; }
.hero-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.1fr 1fr;
  gap: 60px;
  align-items: center;
}
.hero-copy h1 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(2.2rem, 4.4vw, 3.4rem);
  line-height: 1.15;
  margin: 0 0 20px;
}
.hero-copy .lede { max-width: 460px; margin-bottom: 30px; }

.hero-visual {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 4 / 3;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--off-white);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}
.hero-visual-label {
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--grey);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  padding: 10px 18px;
}

/* =========================================================
 LOOP SELECTOR
========================================================= */
.loop-selector { border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding-top: 60px; }
.selector-heading { max-width: 560px; margin-bottom: 46px; }
.selector-heading h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.4vw, 2.6rem);
  margin: 0 0 14px;
}

.loop-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

.loop-card {
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  padding: 40px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 260px;
  transition: background 0.2s ease;
}
.loop-card:hover { background: var(--off-white); }

.loop-card.highlighted { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); }
.loop-card.highlighted:hover { background: #171717; }
.loop-card.highlighted .loop-number { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
.loop-card.highlighted i { color: var(--orange); }
.loop-card.highlighted p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.loop-card.highlighted .loop-link { color: var(--white); border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
.loop-card.highlighted:hover .loop-link { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

.loop-card-top {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: flex-start;
  margin-bottom: auto;
}
.loop-number {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-size: 1rem;
  color: var(--grey);
}
.loop-card-top i { font-size: 1.2rem; color: var(--orange); }

.loop-card h3 {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  margin: 20px 0 10px;
}
.loop-card p {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  line-height: 1.55;
  margin: 0 0 20px;
}

.loop-link {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--black);
  padding-bottom: 2px;
  width: fit-content;
  color: var(--black);
}
.loop-card:hover .loop-link { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .hero-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .hero-visual { order: -1; max-width: 400px; margin: 0 auto; }
  .loop-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .loops-page { padding: 70px 20px 90px; }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — MAGAZINE
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.magazine-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1300px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 0 40px 100px; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 12px 26px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light { border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

.hairline-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
.empty-state, .empty-note { color: var(--grey); }

.panel-link.small {
  display: inline-block;
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--black);
  padding-bottom: 2px;
  color: var(--black);
}
.panel-link.small:hover { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 MASTHEAD — nameplate treatment, thin rules like a print title
========================================================= */
.magazine-masthead {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 90px 20px 40px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 0;
}
.masthead-eyebrow { text-align: center; }
.masthead-title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 6vw, 4.4rem);
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  margin: 0;
  position: relative;
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 0 0 18px;
}
.masthead-title::after {
  content: "";
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: 0;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  width: 60px;
  height: 2px;
  background: var(--orange);
}
.masthead-tagline {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-style: italic;
  color: var(--grey);
  margin: 18px 0 0;
  font-size: 1.05rem;
}

/* =========================================================
 TOOLBAR — filter pills, shared visual language with Categories.
 This is the reference implementation Knowledge/Insights will copy.
========================================================= */
.magazine-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 24px 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  margin-bottom: 60px;
}

.filter-pills {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 10px;
}
.filter-pill {
  display: inline-block;
  padding: 9px 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background: var(--white);
  color: var(--black);
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.03em;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: border-color .2s ease, background .2s ease, color .2s ease;
}
.filter-pill:hover { border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--orange); }
.filter-pill.active { background: var(--black); border-color: var(--black); color: var(--white); }

.search-query-label {
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  color: var(--grey);
  font-style: italic;
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

/* =========================================================
 SPOTLIGHT — the lead featured issue, full editorial treatment
========================================================= */
.spotlight {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1.2fr;
  gap: 60px;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 70px;
}
.spotlight-cover {
  display: block;
  aspect-ratio: 3 / 4;
  background: var(--off-white);
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 30px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.16);
}
.spotlight-cover img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; }

.spotlight-content h2 { margin: 0 0 14px; }
.spotlight-content h2 a {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 500;
  font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3vw, 2.6rem);
  color: var(--black);
  line-height: 1.15;
}
.spotlight-content h2 a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
.spotlight-date { display: block; font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--grey); margin-bottom: 20px; }
.spotlight-excerpt { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); line-height: 1.65; margin-bottom: 26px; max-width: 480px; }

/* =========================================================
 SHELF — secondary featured issues, standing like covers on a shelf
========================================================= */
.shelf-section { margin-bottom: 80px; }
.shelf {
  display: flex;
  gap: 34px;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  padding: 10px 0 0;
}
.shelf-issue { width: 150px; text-align: center; }
.shelf-cover {
  display: block;
  aspect-ratio: 3 / 4;
  background: var(--off-white);
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 14px 26px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
  transition: transform 0.25s ease;
}
.shelf-issue:nth-child(odd) .shelf-cover { transform: rotate(-2deg); }
.shelf-issue:nth-child(even) .shelf-cover { transform: rotate(2deg); }
.shelf-issue:hover .shelf-cover { transform: translateY(-6px) rotate(0deg); }
.shelf-cover img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; }
.shelf-issue h4 { margin: 14px 0 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 0.95rem; }
.shelf-issue h4 a { color: var(--black); }
.shelf-issue:hover h4 a { color: var(--orange); }
.shelf-line { max-width: 700px; margin: 40px auto 0; height: 1px; background: var(--grey-line); }

/* =========================================================
 ARCHIVE — numbered, grouped by category
========================================================= */
.magazine-archive { margin-bottom: 80px; }
.browsing-label { text-align: center; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--grey); margin: -20px 0 40px; }
.browsing-label span { color: var(--orange); font-weight: 600; }

.archive-section { border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding-top: 34px; margin-bottom: 50px; }
.archive-heading { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; margin-bottom: 26px; }
.archive-heading h2 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.5rem; margin: 0; }
.archive-count { font-size: 0.75rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); }

.archive-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(190px, 1fr));
  gap: 34px 24px;
}

.issue-card { display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.issue-cover {
  position: relative;
  display: block;
  aspect-ratio: 3 / 4;
  background: var(--off-white);
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  transition: transform 0.25s ease, box-shadow 0.25s ease;
}
.issue-cover img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; }
.issue-card:hover .issue-cover { transform: translateY(-4px); box-shadow: 0 16px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.12); }

.issue-badge {
  position: absolute;
  top: 10px;
  left: 10px;
  background: rgba(10,10,10,0.75);
  color: var(--white);
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.62rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  padding: 4px 8px;
}

.issue-caption { padding-top: 14px; }
.issue-caption h4 { margin: 0 0 6px; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1rem; }
.issue-caption h4 a { color: var(--black); }
.issue-card:hover .issue-caption h4 a { color: var(--orange); }
.issue-date { font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--grey); }

/* =========================================================
 CLOSING — Popular + Membership
========================================================= */
.magazine-closing {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.3fr 1fr;
  gap: 1px;
  background: var(--grey-line);
  border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}
.closing-block { background: var(--white); padding: 44px; }
.closing-block h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.4rem; margin: 0 0 20px; }
.closing-block.dark { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); }
.closing-block.dark .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); }
.closing-block.dark p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); margin: 0 0 22px; }

.popular-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; }
.popular-item { display: flex; gap: 14px; align-items: center; }
.popular-thumb {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 54px;
  height: 54px;
  background-color: var(--off-white);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
}
.popular-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 0.98rem; color: var(--black); line-height: 1.3; }
.popular-item:hover .popular-title { color: var(--orange); }

/* =========================================================
 RESPONSIVE
========================================================= */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .spotlight { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .spotlight-cover { max-width: 320px; margin: 0 auto; }
  .spotlight-content { text-align: center; }
  .spotlight-excerpt { margin-left: auto; margin-right: auto; }
  .magazine-closing { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .magazine-page { padding: 0 20px 80px; }
  .magazine-toolbar { flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; }
}
```

# FILE: `website\static\css\news.css`

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — NEWS
========================================================= */
:root {
  --black:     #0a0a0a;
  --white:     #ffffff;
  --off-white: #f5f4f1;
  --orange:    #ff5a1f;
  --grey:      #6b6b6b;
  --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

  --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
  --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.news-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1300px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 90px 40px 100px; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 2.8rem); margin: 0 0 14px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; }

.btn-outline {
  display: inline-block;
  border: 1px solid var(--black);
  color: var(--black);
  padding: 11px 24px;
  font-family: var(--font-sans);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.btn-outline:hover { background: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); color: var(--white); }
.btn-outline.light { border-color: var(--white); color: var(--white); }

.hairline-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }

/* HERO */
.news-hero { text-align: center; margin-bottom: 70px; }
.hero-inner { max-width: 700px; margin: 0 auto; }

/* FEATURED */
.news-feature { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 2.2fr 1fr; gap: 1px; background: var(--grey-line); border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); margin-bottom: 60px; }

.feature-card {
  position: relative;
  min-height: 420px;
  display: flex;
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
}
.feature-card::before { content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.3), rgba(10,10,10,.85)); }
.feature-content { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 44px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: flex-end; color: var(--white); }
.feature-content .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); }
.feature-content h2 { margin: 8px 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.9rem; }
.feature-content h2 a { color: var(--white); }
.feature-meta time { font-size: 0.8rem; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }
.feature-content .excerpt { margin-top: 16px; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.85); font-size: 0.95rem; line-height: 1.55; }

.panel-link.small {
  display: inline-block;
  margin-top: 18px;
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--white);
  padding-bottom: 2px;
  color: var(--white);
  width: fit-content;
}
.feature-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

.feature-secondary { display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: var(--white); }
.secondary-title { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); padding: 20px 20px 4px; margin: 0; }
.secondary-item {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-end;
  min-height: 110px;
  padding: 16px 20px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
  background-color: var(--black);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
}
.secondary-item::before { content: ""; position: absolute; inset: 0; background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.2), rgba(10,10,10,.8)); }
.secondary-text { position: relative; z-index: 1; }
.secondary-text h4 { margin: 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1rem; color: var(--white); }
.secondary-text span { font-size: 0.75rem; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }

/* FILTER BAR */
.news-toolbar { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 50px; }
.news-toolbar h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.3rem; margin: 0; }
.category-filters { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 20px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding-bottom: 14px; }
.filter-tab { font-size: 0.8rem; letter-spacing: 0.04em; color: var(--grey); padding-bottom: 6px; }
.filter-tab:hover { color: var(--black); }
.filter-tab.active { color: var(--orange); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--orange); }

/* MAIN LAYOUT */
.news-layout { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 3fr 1.2fr; gap: 60px; }

/* NEWS STREAM */
.news-row { display: flex; gap: 24px; padding: 26px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line); }
.news-thumb {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 180px;
  height: 130px;
  background-color: var(--off-white);
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center;
  display: block;
}
.news-body h4 { margin: 0 0 8px; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.15rem; }
.news-body p { margin-top: 8px; font-size: 0.92rem; color: var(--grey); line-height: 1.5; }
.meta { font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--grey); }
.meta .dot { margin: 0 6px; }
.meta .category { color: var(--orange); }
.empty { color: var(--grey); }

/* ASIDE */
.news-aside { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 40px; }
.aside-block h5 { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.16em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); margin: 0 0 16px; }
.aside-block li { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 3px; padding: 12px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line); }
.aside-block a { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1rem; color: var(--black); }
.aside-block a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
.aside-block span { font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--grey); }

.aside-cta { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); padding: 30px; }
.aside-cta h5 { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); }
.aside-cta p { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.75); font-size: 0.9rem; margin: 0 0 18px; }

/* PAGINATION */
.pagination { margin: 60px 0 20px; text-align: center; }
.pagination a { margin: 0 16px; color: var(--black); font-size: 0.8rem; letter-spacing: 0.06em; text-transform: uppercase; }
.pagination a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
.pagination .current { color: var(--grey); font-size: 0.8rem; }

/* RESPONSIVE */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .news-feature, .news-layout { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .news-row { flex-direction: column; }
  .news-thumb { width: 100%; height: 200px; }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — SEARCH RESULTS
========================================================= */
:root {
    --black:     #0a0a0a;
    --white:     #ffffff;
    --off-white: #f5f4f1;
    --orange:    #ff5a1f;
    --grey:      #6b6b6b;
    --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

    --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.search-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 1280px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 90px 40px 100px; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem); text-align: center; margin: 0 0 14px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; max-width: 560px; margin: 0 auto; }

.panel-link.small {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--black);
    padding-bottom: 2px;
    color: var(--black);
    white-space: nowrap;
}
.panel-link.small:hover { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

.search-header { margin-bottom: 60px; }

.empty-state { text-align: center; padding: 60px 20px; border: 1px solid var(--grey-line); color: var(--grey); margin-bottom: 40px; }
.empty-sub { font-size: 0.9rem; margin-top: 10px; }
.empty-sub a { color: var(--orange); }

.results-section { border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line); padding-top: 34px; margin-bottom: 60px; }
.results-heading { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; margin-bottom: 26px; }
.results-heading h2 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.5rem; margin: 0; }
.results-heading .panel-link.small { color: var(--black); }

.results-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(260px, 1fr));
    gap: 1px;
    background: var(--grey-line);
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
}

.result-card {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 220px;
    background-color: var(--black);
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
}
.result-card::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(10,10,10,.35), rgba(10,10,10,.88));
}
.result-body { position: relative; z-index: 1; padding: 24px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; flex: 1; justify-content: flex-end; }
.result-type { font-size: 0.68rem; letter-spacing: 0.14em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--orange); }
.result-body h3 { margin: 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.15rem; }
.result-body h3 a { color: var(--white); }
.result-card:hover h3 a { color: var(--orange); }
.result-meta { font-size: 0.76rem; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7); }
.result-excerpt { font-size: 0.85rem; color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); line-height: 1.5; margin: 6px 0 0; }
.result-body .panel-link.small { color: var(--white); border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.6); margin-top: 6px; }
.result-card:hover .panel-link.small { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }

@media (max-width: 600px) {
    .search-page { padding: 70px 20px 80px; }
    .results-heading { flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 8px; }
}
```
