# FILE: `website\static\css\style.css`

```css
/* ===== GLOBAL ===== */
html, body {
    height: 100%;
    margin: 0;
    font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif;
    background: #f9f9f9;
    color: #333;
}

a {
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: color 0.3s ease;
}

/* ===== PAGE WRAPPER ===== */
.page-wrapper {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    min-height: 100vh;
    padding-top: 140px; /* Space for fixed header */
}

/* ===== HEADER ===== */
.site-header {
    background: #fff;
    border-bottom: 3px solid #2E7D32;
    font-family: 'Montserrat', sans-serif;
    box-shadow: 0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.05);
    position: fixed;
    top: 0;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    z-index: 9999;
}

/* Top Bar */
.top-bar {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    padding: 15px 50px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 15px;
}

/* Logo */
.logo h1 {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: 2em;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: #001a0b;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 1px;
    transition: color 0.3s ease;
}

.logo .tagline {
    font-size: 0.85em;
    color: #291717;
    margin: 3px 0 0 0;
    transition: color 0.3s ease;
}

.logo-link {
    text-decoration: none;
    color: inherit;
    display: inline-block;
}

.logo-link:hover h1,
.logo-link:hover .tagline {
    color: #00b894;
}

/* Search Box */
.search-box {
    flex: 1;
    max-width: 400px;
    margin: 0 20px;
}

.search-box form {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    border: 2px solid #2E7D32;
    border-radius: 50px;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: #fff;
    transition: border-color 0.3s ease;
}

.search-box input {
    flex: 1;
    padding: 10px 15px;
    border: none;
    outline: none;
    font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif;
    font-size: 0.95em;
}

.search-box button {
    padding: 10px 18px;
    background: #2E7D32;
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background 0.3s ease;
}

.search-box button:hover {
    background: #F9A825;
}

/* Auth & Social Links */
.auth-socials {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 15px;
}

.auth-links {
    display: flex;
    gap: 10px;
}

.auth-links a {
    font-size: 0.9em;
    color: #2E7D32;
    font-weight: 600;
}

.auth-links a:hover {
    color: #F9A825;
}

.social-links a {
    color: #2E7D32;
    margin-left: 8px;
    font-size: 1.1em;
}

.social-links a:hover {
    color: #F9A825;
}

/* ===== MAIN NAV ===== */
.main-nav {
    background: #2E7D32;
    padding: 0 20px;
}

.main-nav ul {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 5px;
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    list-style: none;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
}

/* Nav links */
.main-nav a {
    display: block;
    padding: 14px 22px;
    text-decoration: none;
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.5px;
    border-radius: 30px;
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.main-nav a:hover {
    background: #F9A825;
    color: #000;
}

.main-nav a.active {
    background: #fff;
    color: #2E7D32;
    border: 2px solid #F9A825;
}

/* Dropdown Menu */
.dropdown {
    position: relative;
}

.dropdown-toggle::after {
    content: " \25BE";
    font-size: 0.7em;
    margin-left: 5px;
}

.dropdown-menu {
    display: none !important;
    position: absolute;
    top: 100%;
    left: 0;
    background-color: #fff;
    min-width: 300px;
    padding: 10px;
    box-shadow: 0 3px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
    border-radius: 5px;
    z-index: 1000;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    gap: 0;
}

.dropdown-menu li {
    padding: 8px 15px;
}

.dropdown-menu li a {
    color: #333;
    display: block;
}

.dropdown-menu li a:hover {
    background-color: #00b894;
    color: #fff;
    border-radius: 3px;
}

.dropdown:hover > .dropdown-menu {
    display: grid !important;
}

.dropdown > a {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
}

/* ===== MAIN CONTENT ===== */
.container {
    flex: 1;
    max-width: 1200px;
    margin: 30px auto;
    padding: 0 20px;
}

/* Messages */
.messages {
    list-style: none;
    padding: 0;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.messages li {
    padding: 10px 15px;
    margin-bottom: 10px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    font-size: 0.95em;
}

.messages li.success { background: #d4edda; color: #155724; }
.messages li.error   { background: #f8d7da; color: #721c24; }
.messages li.warning { background: #fff3cd; color: #856404; }

/* ===== HERO SECTION ===== */
.hero {
    background: url('../images/hero-bg.jpg') no-repeat center center/cover;
    text-align: center;
    padding: 120px 20px 80px;
    color: #fff;
    position: relative;
}

.hero h1 {
    font-size: 2.8em;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    font-family: 'Lora', serif;
}

.hero p {
    font-size: 1.2em;
    margin-bottom: 30px;
}

.hero .btn {
    background: #2E7D32;
    color: #fff;
    padding: 12px 25px;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-radius: 50px;
    margin: 0 10px;
    display: inline-block;
    transition: background 0.3s ease;
}

.hero .btn:hover {
    background: #F9A825;
    color: #000;
}

/* ===== DIRECTORY CTA ===== */
.directory-cta {
    background: #f0f0f0;
    padding: 30px 20px;
    text-align: center;
    margin: 40px 0;
    border-radius: 10px;
}

.directory-cta h2 {
    margin-bottom: 20px;
    font-size: 1.8em;
    color: #2E7D32;
}

.directory-cta a.btn {
    background: #2E7D32;
    color: #fff;
    padding: 12px 25px;
    margin: 10px;
    border-radius: 50px;
    font-weight: 600;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    display: inline-block;
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.directory-cta a.btn:hover {
    background: #F9A825;
    color: #000;
}

/* ===== FOOTER ===== */
.site-footer {
    background: #001601;
    color: #fff;
    font-family: 'Open Sans', sans-serif;
    padding: 40px 50px 20px;
}

.site-footer a {
    color: #A5D6A7;
}

.site-footer a:hover {
    color: #F9A825;
}

.footer-top {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 30px;
    margin-bottom: 30px;
}

.footer-column {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 200px;
}

.footer-column h4 {
    margin-bottom: 12px;
    font-size: 1.1em;
    font-weight: 700;
}

.footer-column p {
    margin: 0;
    line-height: 1.6;
    font-size: 0.95em;
}

.footer-bottom {
    border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
    padding-top: 15px;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    font-size: 0.85em;
    color: #C8E6C9;
}

/* ===== RESPONSIVE ===== */
@media (max-width: 900px) {
    .top-bar {
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .search-box {
        max-width: 100%;
        width: 100%;
        margin: 10px 0;
    }

    .auth-socials {
        flex-direction: row;
        justify-content: space-between;
        width: 100%;
    }

    .main-nav ul {
        flex-direction: column;
        width: 100%;
        align-items: flex-start;
    }

    .main-nav a {
        width: 100%;
        border-radius: 0;
    }

    .footer-top {
        flex-direction: column;
    }

    .footer-bottom {
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 5px;
        text-align: center;
    }

    .hero {
        padding: 80px 20px 50px;
    }

    .hero h1 {
        font-size: 2.2em;
    }

    .hero p {
        font-size: 1em;
    }

    .directory-cta h2 {
        font-size: 1.5em;
    }

    .directory-cta a.btn {
        padding: 10px 20px;
        font-size: 0.9em;
    }
}
```

```css
/* =========================================================
   CALL SOSO — SUPPORT
========================================================= */
:root {
    --black:     #0a0a0a;
    --white:     #ffffff;
    --off-white: #f5f4f1;
    --orange:    #ff5a1f;
    --grey:      #6b6b6b;
    --grey-line: rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

    --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
    --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }

.support-page { font-family: var(--font-sans); color: var(--black); max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 90px 40px 100px; }

.eyebrow { font-size: 0.72rem; letter-spacing: 0.22em; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--grey); display: block; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.eyebrow.center { text-align: center; }
.section-title { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.6rem); text-align: center; margin: 0 0 14px; }
.lede { font-size: 1rem; color: var(--grey); }
.lede.center { text-align: center; max-width: 560px; margin: 0 auto; }

.support-hero { margin-bottom: 60px; }

/* FUNDING OPTIONS */
.funding-options {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    gap: 1px;
    background: var(--grey-line);
    border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    margin-bottom: 80px;
}
.fund-card { background: var(--black); color: var(--white); padding: 44px; text-align: center; }
.fund-card .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.65); }
.fund-card h3 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: 1.5rem; margin: 0 0 20px; }
.fund-card:hover { background: var(--orange); }

.panel-link.small {
    display: inline-block;
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--white);
    padding-bottom: 2px;
    color: var(--white);
}

/* CONTRIBUTIONS */
.contributions { margin-bottom: 40px; }

.contrib-list { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 40px 0 0; }
.contrib-item {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 16px 4px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    transition: color 0.15s ease, padding-left 0.15s ease;
}
.contrib-item.empty { color: var(--grey); justify-content: center; }

.contrib-name { font-family: var(--font-display); font-size: 1.05rem; }
.contrib-detail { font-size: 0.88rem; color: var(--grey); }

.contrib-item.is-hovered { padding-left: 10px; }
.contrib-item.is-hovered .contrib-name { color: var(--black); font-weight: 600; }
.contrib-item.is-hovered.major-gift .contrib-name { color: var(--orange); }

/* RESPONSIVE */
@media (max-width: 700px) {
    .funding-options { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .contrib-item { flex-direction: column; }
}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\admin\base_site.html`

```html
{% extends "admin/base_site.html" %}
{% load static %}

{# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
   WHERE THIS GOES:
   Create a project-level templates directory (sibling to manage.py is
   fine), e.g.  C:\...\callsoso\templates\admin\base_site.html
   and save this file there.

   Then in settings.py, make sure that folder is in TEMPLATES[0]['DIRS']:

       TEMPLATES = [
           {
               ...
               'DIRS': [BASE_DIR / 'templates'],   # <-- add/confirm this
               'APP_DIRS': True,
               ...
           },
       ]

   This override MUST live in a DIRS-listed folder (not just any app's
   templates/ folder) to reliably win over Django's own admin templates,
   regardless of INSTALLED_APPS ordering.
--------------------------------------------------------------------- #}

{% block title %}{{ title }} — Call Soso Admin{% endblock %}

{% block branding %}
<h1 id="site-name">
    <a href="{% url 'admin:index' %}">Call Soso <span class="admin-suffix">Admin</span></a>
</h1>
{% endblock %}

{% block nav-global %}{% endblock %}

{% block extrastyle %}
{{ block.super }}
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:wght@500;600&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'admin/css/call_soso_admin.css' %}">
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\about.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}About Us — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/about.css' %}">

<!-- HERO -->
<header class="about-hero" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/about-hero.jpg' %}');">
    <div class="about-hero-inner">
        <p class="eyebrow center">About Call Soso</p>
        <h1 class="section-title">Turning surplus into value through circular systems.</h1>
        <p class="lede center">Community creativity and responsible design, rooted in Milton Keynes.</p>
        <p class="hero-microcopy">&ldquo;Waste is only waste when imagination stops.&rdquo;</p>
    </div>
</header>

<div class="about-container">

    <!-- WHO WE ARE -->
    <section class="about-section">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Who We Are</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">A circular economy venture</h2>
        <p class="lede center">
            Call Soso is a Milton Keynes–based circular economy venture working at the intersection of
            corporate surplus, creative reuse, and community impact. We capture materials that would
            otherwise be discarded and redirect them into meaningful second lives.
        </p>
        <p class="lede center">
            From cardboard and textiles to foam and product returns, we work with businesses, upcyclers,
            designers, and educators to transform waste into value — socially, environmentally, and economically.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- ROOTED MANIFESTO -->
    <section class="about-section dark-block">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Manifesto</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">The Rooted Manifesto</h2>
        <p class="lede center">
            We honour materials, edible and otherwise. Everything has a second life. We treat surplus as
            intelligence, culture, and possibility. Circularity isn't compliance, it's creativity.
            We build systems, not scraps.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- HOW IT WORKS -->
    <section class="about-section">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Process</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">How It Works</h2>

        <div class="about-cards">
            <div class="about-card">
                <span class="card-num">01</span>
                <h3>Capture</h3>
                <p>We partner with organisations to identify surplus materials across their operations —
                   packaging, textiles, offcuts, returns, and more.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="about-card">
                <span class="card-num">02</span>
                <h3>Match</h3>
                <p>Materials are matched with internal teams, makers, creatives, and community groups
                   who can reuse them effectively.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="about-card">
                <span class="card-num">03</span>
                <h3>Transform</h3>
                <p>Surplus becomes new products, installations, learning tools, or resources — extending
                   material life and reducing waste.</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- IMPACT -->
    <section class="about-section">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Our Impact</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">What This Adds Up To</h2>

        <div class="impact-grid">
            <div class="impact-item">
                <span class="impact-number">10%</span>
                <p>of profits reinvested into community workshops</p>
            </div>
            <div class="impact-item">
                <span class="impact-number">1000s</span>
                <p>of materials diverted from landfill</p>
            </div>
            <div class="impact-item">
                <span class="impact-number">&infin;</span>
                <p>creative reuse possibilities unlocked</p>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- FOUNDING STORIES -->
    <section class="about-section dark-block">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Origins</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">Founding Stories</h2>

        <div class="founders-grid">
            <div>
                <h4>Sybil — Founder &amp; Creative Director</h4>
                <p>Sybil built Call Soso from a clear instinct: everything we overlook — materials, food,
                   ideas, people — can reshape a city.</p>
            </div>
            <div>
                <h4>Nyasha — Co-Founder</h4>
                <p>Nyasha provides precision, structure, and operational flow to bring the ecosystem to life.</p>
            </div>
        </div>

        <form method="POST" action="{% url 'website:home' %}" class="founders-form">
            {% csrf_token %}
            <input type="email" name="email" placeholder="Enter your email" required>
            <button type="submit" class="btn-outline light">Join the Founders List</button>
        </form>
    </section>

    <!-- FOUNDATION -->
    <section class="about-section">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Give Back</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">The Call Soso Foundation</h2>
        <p class="lede center">
            We reinvest 10% of our profits into the Call Soso Foundation, supporting workshops,
            educational programmes, and hands-on learning experiences for communities.
        </p>
        <p class="lede center">
            These initiatives empower people to understand materials, develop creative confidence,
            and participate directly in the circular economy.
        </p>
    </section>

    <!-- CTA -->
    <section class="about-cta">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Get Involved</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">Work With Us</h2>
        <p class="lede center">
            Whether you're a business with surplus, a designer, educator, or community group —
            we'd love to collaborate.
        </p>
        <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="btn-outline light">Get in Touch</a>
    </section>

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\article_detail.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}{{ article.title }} – Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/article_detail.css' %}">

<!-- HERO -->
<header class="article-hero">
    <h1>{{ article.title }}</h1>

    {% if article.published_date %}
    <p class="date">{{ article.published_date|date:"jS F, Y" }}</p>
    {% endif %}

    {% if article.categories %}
    <p class="categories">
        {% for category in article.categories %}
            {{ category }}{% if not forloop.last %} / {% endif %}
        {% endfor %}
    </p>
    {% endif %}
</header>

<!-- FEATURE IMAGE -->
{% if article.display_image %}
<div class="article-image">
    <img src="{{ article.display_image }}" alt="{{ article.title }}" loading="lazy">
</div>
{% endif %}

<!-- ARTICLE CONTENT -->
<article class="article-body">
    {% if article.excerpt %}
    <p class="excerpt">{{ article.excerpt }}</p>
    {% endif %}

    {% if article.summary %}
    <p class="summary">{{ article.summary|linebreaks }}</p>
    {% endif %}

    {% if article.body %}
    <div class="body-content">{{ article.body|safe }}</div>
    {% endif %}
</article>

<!-- RELATED ARTICLES -->
{% if related_articles %}
<section class="related-articles">
    <h3>Related Articles</h3>
    <ul>
        {% for rel in related_articles %}
        <li>
            <a href="{{ rel.get_absolute_url }}">
                {{ rel.title }}
            </a>
            {% if rel.published_date %}
            <span class="date">{{ rel.published_date|date:"jS F, Y" }}</span>
            {% endif %}
        </li>
        {% endfor %}
    </ul>
</section>
{% endif %}

{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\base.html`

```html
{% load static %}
{% now "Y" as current_year %}
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>{% block title %}Call Soso — Circularity in Resonance{% endblock %}</title>
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">

    <!-- Google Fonts: serif display + tracked sans utility -->
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:ital,wght@0,400;0,500;0,600;1,400;1,500&family=Inter:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

    <!-- Font Awesome (kept for icon usage in social/nav) -->
    <link href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.5.0/css/all.min.css" rel="stylesheet">

    <!-- NOTE: the old static/css/style.css is no longer linked here — it predates
         this redesign and clashes on shared class names (.site-header, .main-nav,
         .dropdown-menu, .hero, .site-footer, .directory-cta, etc). Everything it
         did is now handled below. Keep the file on disk until any other pages
         still depending on it are ported over, just don't link it from here. -->

    <style>
        /* =========================================================
           CALL SOSO — DESIGN TOKENS
           Editorial, high-contrast, warehouse/soil duotone accents.
           Black / white / signal-orange. Serif display, tracked sans.
        ========================================================= */
        :root {
            --black:        #0a0a0a;
            --white:        #ffffff;
            --off-white:    #f5f4f1;
            --orange:       #ff5a1f;
            --grey:         #6b6b6b;
            --grey-line:    rgba(10,10,10,0.1);

            --font-display: 'Cormorant Garamond', Georgia, serif;
            --font-sans:    'Inter', -apple-system, sans-serif;

            --header-h: 84px;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; }

        html, body {
            height: 100%;
            margin: 0;
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            background: var(--white);
            color: var(--black);
            -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
        }

        a { text-decoration: none; color: inherit; transition: color .25s ease, background .25s ease, opacity .25s ease; }

        .eyebrow {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            letter-spacing: 0.22em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            color: var(--grey);
        }

        /* ===== PAGE WRAPPER ===== */
        .page-wrapper {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            min-height: 100vh;
            padding-top: var(--header-h);
        }

        /* ===== HEADER ===== */
        .site-header {
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: var(--header-h);
            z-index: 9999;
            background: var(--white);
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        }

        .top-bar {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            height: var(--header-h);
            padding: 0 40px;
            gap: 24px;
        }

        .logo-link { display: flex; flex-direction: column; line-height: 1; }
        .logo-link h1 {
            margin: 0;
            font-family: var(--font-display);
            font-weight: 600;
            font-size: 1.6rem;
            letter-spacing: 0.04em;
            color: var(--black);
        }
        .logo-link .tagline {
            margin-top: 3px;
            font-family: var(--font-display);
            font-style: italic;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--grey);
        }

        /* Primary nav — flat, tracked-out, no pills/backgrounds */
        .main-nav ul {
            list-style: none;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 30px;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
        }
        .main-nav a {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.14em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            color: var(--black);
            padding: 6px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid transparent;
        }
        .main-nav a.active,
        .main-nav a:hover {
            color: var(--orange);
            border-bottom-color: var(--orange);
        }

            /* ==========================================
            CALL SOSO — PREMIUM CATEGORY MEGA DROPDOWN
        ========================================== */
        
        .dropdown{
            position:relative;
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu{
        
            position:absolute;
        
            top:calc(100% + 18px);
        
            left:50%;
        
            transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(-10px);
        
            width:1050px;
        
            max-width:95vw;
        
            padding:28px;
        
            background:var(--white);
        
            border:1px solid var(--grey-line);
        
            border-radius:20px;
        
            box-shadow:
                0 22px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.12);
        
            display:grid;
        
            grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);
        
            gap:18px;
        
            list-style:none;
        
            margin:0;
        
            opacity:0;
        
            visibility:hidden;
        
            pointer-events:none;
        
            transition:
                opacity .25s ease,
                transform .25s ease,
                visibility .25s ease;
        
            z-index:9999;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown:hover>.dropdown-menu,
        .dropdown.open>.dropdown-menu,
        .dropdown:focus-within>.dropdown-menu{
        
            opacity:1;
        
            visibility:visible;
        
            pointer-events:auto;
        
            transform:translateX(-50%) translateY(0);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu::before{
        
            content:"";
        
            position:absolute;
        
            top:-9px;
        
            left:50%;
        
            width:18px;
        
            height:18px;
        
            background:#fff;
        
            transform:translateX(-50%) rotate(45deg);
        
            border-top:1px solid var(--grey-line);
        
            border-left:1px solid var(--grey-line);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu>li{
        
            list-style:none;
        
            margin:0;
        
            padding:0;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a{
        
            display:flex;
        
            flex-direction:column;
        
            justify-content:center;
        
            align-items:flex-start;
        
            gap:6px;
        
            width:100%;
        
            min-height:84px;
        
            padding:18px;
        
            border-radius:12px;
        
            background:#fff;
        
            border:1px solid transparent;
        
            transition:all .22s ease;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a:hover{
        
            background:#faf8f4;
        
            border-color:#ece6dc;
        
            transform:translateY(-2px);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a.active{
        
            background:#faf8f4;
        
            border-color:#ece6dc;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-title{
        
            font-family:var(--font-display);
        
            font-size:1.08rem;
        
            font-weight:500;
        
            line-height:1.2;
        
            color:var(--black);
        
            transition:.25s;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-subtitle{
        
            font-family:var(--font-sans);
        
            font-size:.70rem;
        
            letter-spacing:.14em;
        
            text-transform:uppercase;
        
            color:var(--grey);
        
            transition:.25s;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a:hover .dropdown-title{
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a:hover .dropdown-subtitle{
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a.active .dropdown-title{
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-menu li a.active .dropdown-subtitle{
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-toggle{
        
            appearance:none;
        
            background:none;
        
            border:none;
        
            outline:none;
        
            cursor:pointer;
        
            padding:6px 0;
        
            margin:0;
        
            font-family:var(--font-sans);
        
            font-size:.75rem;
        
            font-weight:500;
        
            letter-spacing:.14em;
        
            text-transform:uppercase;
        
            color:var(--black);
        
            border-bottom:1px solid transparent;
        
            transition:.25s;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-toggle:hover,
        .dropdown-toggle.active,
        .dropdown-toggle:focus{
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
            border-bottom-color:var(--orange);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-toggle::after{
        
            content:" ▾";
        
            font-size:.65rem;
        
            margin-left:6px;
        
            transition:transform .25s ease;
        
        }
        
        .dropdown.open .dropdown-toggle::after,
        .dropdown:hover .dropdown-toggle::after{
        
            transform:rotate(180deg);
        
        }
        
        .dropdown-empty{
        
            grid-column:1/-1;
        
            display:flex;
        
            justify-content:center;
        
            align-items:center;
        
            min-height:80px;
        
            color:var(--grey);
        
            font-size:.9rem;
        
        }
        
        .view-all-row{
        
            grid-column:1/-1;
        
            margin-top:10px;
        
            padding-top:20px;
        
            border-top:1px solid var(--grey-line);
        
        }
        
        .view-all{
        
            display:flex !important;
        
            justify-content:center;
        
            align-items:center;
        
            min-height:50px;
        
            font-family:var(--font-sans);
        
            font-size:.74rem;
        
            font-weight:600;
        
            letter-spacing:.14em;
        
            text-transform:uppercase;
        
            color:var(--orange);
        
            background:none !important;
        
            border:none !important;
        
        }
        
        .view-all:hover{
        
            color:var(--black);
        
            background:none !important;
        
            transform:none !important;
        
        }
        
        @media (max-width:1100px){
        
            .dropdown-menu{
        
                width:760px;
        
                grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);
        
            }
        
        }
        
        @media (max-width:768px){
        
            .dropdown-menu{
        
                position:fixed;
        
                top:84px;
        
                left:50%;
        
                transform:translateX(-50%);
        
                width:94vw;
        
                max-height:72vh;
        
                overflow-y:auto;
        
                grid-template-columns:1fr;
        
            }
        
        }

        /* ---------- Mobile ---------- */

        @media (max-width:768px){

            .dropdown-menu{

                position:fixed;

                top:84px;

                left:50%;

                transform:translateX(-50%);

                width:94vw;

                max-height:70vh;

                overflow-y:auto;

                grid-template-columns:1fr;

            }

        }

        /* Right-hand utility cluster: search / auth / social */
        .utility-cluster { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 22px; }

        .search-box input {
            padding: 6px 0;
            border: none;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
            outline: none;
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            width: 120px;
            background: transparent;
        }
        .search-box button {
            background: none;
            border: none;
            color: var(--black);
            cursor: pointer;
            padding: 0 0 0 6px;
        }
        .search-box button:hover { color: var(--orange); }

        .auth-links a {
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.1em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-left: 18px;
        }
        .auth-links a:hover { color: var(--orange); }
        .auth-links a.signup { color: var(--orange); }

        .social-links a { margin-left: 14px; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--black); }
        .social-links a:hover { color: var(--orange); }

        /* ===== MAIN CONTENT ===== */
        main.container {
            flex: 1;
            width: 100%;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        /* Messages */
        .messages { list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; }
        .messages li { padding: 12px 40px; font-size: 0.85rem; letter-spacing: 0.02em; }
        .messages li.success { background: #eef7ee; color: #1c5c2e; }
        .messages li.error   { background: #fdecea; color: #7a1e2a; }
        .messages li.warning { background: #fff5e6; color: #6b4a00; }

        /* ===== GENERIC HERO (used by pages that don't ship their own) ===== */
        .hero {
            padding: 100px 40px;
            text-align: center;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        }
        .hero h1 {
            font-family: var(--font-display);
            font-weight: 500;
            font-size: clamp(2.4rem, 5vw, 4.2rem);
            letter-spacing: 0.01em;
            margin: 0 0 20px;
        }
        .hero p {
            font-family: var(--font-sans);
            font-size: 1rem;
            color: var(--grey);
            margin: 0 0 30px;
        }
        .hero .btn {
            display: inline-block;
            background: var(--black);
            color: var(--white);
            padding: 13px 30px;
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            letter-spacing: 0.16em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
        }
        .hero .btn:hover { background: var(--orange); }


        /* ===== FOOTER CTA — editorial card strip ===== */
        .footer-cta-strip {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
            background: var(--black);
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card {
            position: relative;
            padding: 52px 32px 44px;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: flex-start;
            border-right: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07);
            transition: background .3s ease;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card:last-child { border-right: none; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0; left: 0; right: 0;
            height: 3px;
            transition: height .3s ease;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.support::before  { background: #4caf81; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.contact::before  { background: #5b8dd9; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.business::before { background: var(--orange); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.creators::before { background: var(--white); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.04); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card:hover::before { height: 5px; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card .eyebrow {
            font-size: 0.66rem;
            letter-spacing: 0.22em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-bottom: 18px;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.support  .eyebrow { color: #4caf81; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.contact  .eyebrow { color: #5b8dd9; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.business .eyebrow { color: var(--orange); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.creators .eyebrow { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card h3 {
            font-family: var(--font-display);
            font-weight: 500;
            font-size: 1.55rem;
            line-height: 1.2;
            color: var(--white);
            margin: 0 0 10px;
            flex: 1;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card .sub {
            font-size: 0.78rem;
            line-height: 1.55;
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.45);
            margin: 0 0 28px;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card .arrow-link {
            display: inline-flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 8px;
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            font-weight: 500;
            letter-spacing: 0.14em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            color: var(--white);
            padding-bottom: 2px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
            transition: border-color .25s, color .25s;
        }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.support  .arrow-link:hover { color: #4caf81;       border-color: #4caf81; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.contact  .arrow-link:hover { color: #5b8dd9;       border-color: #5b8dd9; }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.business .arrow-link:hover { color: var(--orange); border-color: var(--orange); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card.creators .arrow-link:hover { color: var(--off-white); border-color: var(--off-white); }
        .footer-cta-strip .cta-card .arrow-link .arr { font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1; }

        .footer-bottom {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 22px 40px;
            background: var(--black);
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07);
        }
        .footer-location {
            font-size: 0.72rem;
            letter-spacing: 0.1em;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            color: var(--white);
        }

        @media (max-width: 980px) {
            .footer-cta-strip { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
            .footer-cta-strip .cta-card { border-right: none; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07); }
            .footer-cta-strip .cta-card:nth-child(odd) { border-right: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.07); }
        }
        @media (max-width: 600px) {
            .footer-cta-strip { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
            .footer-cta-strip .cta-card { border-right: none !important; }
        }
    </style>
    {% block extra_css %}{% endblock %}
</head>
<body>

<div class="page-wrapper">

    <!-- ===== HEADER ===== -->
    <header class="site-header">
        <div class="top-bar">

            <div class="logo">
                <a href="{% url 'website:home' %}" class="logo-link">
                    <h1>Call Soso</h1>
                    <p class="tagline">Circularity in Resonance</p>
                </a>
            </div>

            <nav class="main-nav">
                <ul>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'website:home' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/' %}active{% endif %}">Home</a></li>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'directory:directory_home' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/directory/' %}active{% endif %}">Directory</a></li>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/insights/' %}active{% endif %}">Stories</a></li>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'website:knowledge' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/knowledge/' %}active{% endif %}">Services</a></li>
                    <li class="dropdown">

                        <button
                            type="button"
                            class="dropdown-toggle {% if request.path == '/categories/' %}active{% endif %}">
                            Categories
                        </button>
                    
                        <ul class="dropdown-menu">
                    
                            {% for category in nav_categories %}
                    
                            <li>
                    
                                <a
                                    href="{% url 'website:categories' %}?category={{ category.slug }}"
                                    class="{% if request.GET.category == category.slug %}active{% endif %}">
                    
                                    <span class="dropdown-title">
                                        {{ category.name }}
                                    </span>
                    
                                    <span class="dropdown-subtitle">
                                        Stories • Services • Magazine
                                    </span>
                    
                                </a>
                    
                            </li>
                    
                            {% empty %}
                    
                            <li class="dropdown-empty">
                                No categories available.
                            </li>
                    
                            {% endfor %}
                    
                            <li class="view-all-row">
                    
                                <a
                                    href="{% url 'website:categories' %}"
                                    class="view-all">
                    
                                    Browse All Categories →
                    
                                </a>
                    
                            </li>
                    
                        </ul>
                    
                    </li>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'website:about' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/about/' %}active{% endif %}">About</a></li>
                    <li><a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="{% if request.path == '/contact/' %}active{% endif %}">Contact</a></li>
                </ul>
            </nav>

            <div class="utility-cluster">
                <div class="search-box">
                    <form method="get" action="{% url 'website:search' %}">
                        <input type="text" name="q" placeholder="Search">
                        <button type="submit"><i class="fas fa-search"></i></button>
                    </form>
                </div>

                <div class="auth-links">
                    {% if user.is_authenticated %}
                        {% if user.is_superuser %}
                            <a href="{% url 'admin:index' %}">Dashboard</a>
                        {% else %}
                            <a href="{% url 'directory:directory_home' %}">Dashboard</a>
                        {% endif %}
                        <a href="{% url 'logout' %}">Logout</a>
                    {% else %}
                        <a href="{% url 'login' %}">Login</a>
                        <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="signup">Sign Up</a>
                    {% endif %}
                </div>

                <div class="social-links">
                    <a href="https://www.instagram.com/callsoso_/"><i class="fab fa-instagram"></i></a>
                    <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/callsoso_/"><i class="fab fa-linkedin"></i></a>
                    <a href="https://www.twitter.com/callsoso_/"><i class="fab fa-twitter"></i></a>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- ===== MAIN CONTENT ===== -->
    <main class="container">
        {% if messages %}
            <ul class="messages">
                {% for message in messages %}
                    <li class="{{ message.tags }}">{{ message }}</li>
                {% endfor %}
            </ul>
        {% endif %}

        {% block hero %}{% endblock %}
        {% block content %}{% endblock %}
    </main>

    <!-- ===== FOOTER ===== -->
    <footer class="site-footer">

        <div class="footer-cta-strip">

            <div class="cta-card support">
                <span class="eyebrow">Community</span>
                <h3>Support Us</h3>
                <p class="sub">Back the mission via our Kickstarter portal and help grow the circular economy.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'website:support' %}" class="arrow-link">Kickstarter Portal <span class="arr">→</span></a>
            </div>

            <div class="cta-card contact">
                <span class="eyebrow">Collaborate</span>
                <h3>Get in Touch</h3>
                <p class="sub">Partner with us, pitch a story, or explore collaboration opportunities.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="arrow-link">Contact Us <span class="arr">→</span></a>
            </div>

            <div class="cta-card business">
                <span class="eyebrow">For Business</span>
                <h3>Get an Audit</h3>
                <p class="sub">Request a circularity audit and discover where your surplus can flow next.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="arrow-link">Start Audit <span class="arr">→</span></a>
            </div>

            <div class="cta-card creators">
                <span class="eyebrow">For Creators</span>
                <h3>Join the Pulse</h3>
                <p class="sub">List your practice, connect with surplus, and reach the circular community.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="arrow-link">Join Now <span class="arr">→</span></a>
            </div>

        </div>

        <div class="footer-bottom">
            <span class="footer-location">Milton Keynes / Circular Logistics / © {{ current_year }} Call Soso</span>
        </div>

    </footer>

</div> <!-- /.page-wrapper -->


{% block modals %}{% endblock %}

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    var dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown');

    dropdowns.forEach(function (dropdown) {
        var toggle = dropdown.querySelector('.dropdown-toggle');
        if (!toggle) return;

        toggle.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var isOpen = dropdown.classList.contains('open');
            dropdowns.forEach(function (d) { d.classList.remove('open'); });
            if (!isOpen) dropdown.classList.add('open');
        });
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        if (!e.target.closest('.dropdown')) {
            dropdowns.forEach(function (d) { d.classList.remove('open'); });
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            dropdowns.forEach(function (d) { d.classList.remove('open'); });
        }
    });
});
</script>

</body>
</html>
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\categories.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Browse by Category — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/categories.css' %}">

<div class="categories-page">

    <header class="categories-header">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Explore</p>

        <h1 class="section-title">
            Browse by Category
        </h1>

        <p class="lede center">
            Discover insights, resources and magazine issues organised into
            the key themes driving the Call Soso circular economy ecosystem.
        </p>
    </header>

    {% if category_data %}

    <div class="categories-grid">

        {% for entry in category_data %}

        <article class="category-tile">

            <div class="category-header">
        
                <div class="category-heading">
        
                    <h2>
                        <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}?category={{ entry.category.slug }}">
                            {{ entry.category.name }}
                        </a>
                    </h2>
        
                    <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}?category={{ entry.category.slug }}"
                       class="browse-category">
        
                        Browse everything
                        <span>&rarr;</span>
        
                    </a>
        
                </div>
        
                <span class="category-total">
        
                    {{ entry.story_count|add:entry.service_count|add:entry.issue_count }}
        
                    Items
        
                </span>
        
            </div>
        
            <div class="tile-links">
        
                <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}?category={{ entry.category.slug }}"
                   class="tile-link {% if not entry.story_count %}muted{% endif %}">
        
                    <span class="tile-count">
                        {{ entry.story_count }}
                    </span>
        
                    Insight{{ entry.story_count|pluralize }}
        
                </a>
        
                <a href="{% url 'website:knowledge' %}?category={{ entry.category.slug }}"
                   class="tile-link {% if not entry.service_count %}muted{% endif %}">
        
                    <span class="tile-count">
                        {{ entry.service_count }}
                    </span>
        
                    Resource{{ entry.service_count|pluralize }}
        
                </a>
        
                <a href="{% url 'website:magazine' %}?category={{ entry.category.slug }}"
                   class="tile-link {% if not entry.issue_count %}muted{% endif %}">
        
                    <span class="tile-count">
                        {{ entry.issue_count }}
                    </span>
        
                    Magazine Issue{{ entry.issue_count|pluralize }}
        
                </a>
        
            </div>
        
        </article>

        {% endfor %}

    </div>

    {% else %}

    <div class="empty-state">

        <p>No categories available yet.</p>

        <p class="empty-sub">
            Categories created in the admin dashboard will automatically
            appear here once content has been assigned to them.
        </p>

    </div>

    {% endif %}

</div>

{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\contact.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Contact — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/contact.css' %}">

<main class="contact-page">

    <!-- HERO -->
    <header class="contact-header">
        <div class="hero-inner">
            <p class="eyebrow center">Reach Out</p>
            <h1 class="section-title">Let's Work in Resonance</h1>
            <p class="lede center">Reach out for partnerships, collaborations, commissions, or to join our circular movement.</p>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- LAYOUT -->
    <section class="contact-layout">

        <!-- FORM -->
        <div class="contact-form-section">
            <form method="POST" novalidate class="contact-form">
                {% csrf_token %}

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="name">Name <span class="required">*</span></label>
                    <input type="text" name="name" id="name" required placeholder="Your full name" value="{{ request.POST.name|default:'' }}">
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="email">Email <span class="required">*</span></label>
                    <input type="email" name="email" id="email" required placeholder="you@example.com" value="{{ request.POST.email|default:'' }}">
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="organization">Organization</label>
                    <input type="text" name="organization" id="organization" placeholder="Optional" value="{{ request.POST.organization|default:'' }}">
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="inquiry_type">Inquiry Type <span class="required">*</span></label>
                    <select name="inquiry_type" id="inquiry_type" required>
                        <option value="General" {% if request.POST.inquiry_type == "General" %}selected{% endif %}>General</option>
                        <option value="Virtual hub audit" {% if request.POST.inquiry_type == "Virtual hub audit" %}selected{% endif %}>Virtual hub audit</option>
                        <option value="Collaboration / Partnership" {% if request.POST.inquiry_type == "Collaboration / Partnership" %}selected{% endif %}>Collaboration / Partnership</option>
                        <option value="Commission / Project" {% if request.POST.inquiry_type == "Commission / Project" %}selected{% endif %}>Commission / Project</option>
                        <option value="Other" {% if request.POST.inquiry_type == "Other" %}selected{% endif %}>Other</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="message">Message <span class="required">*</span></label>
                    <textarea name="message" id="message" rows="6" required placeholder="Write your message here...">{{ request.POST.message|default:'' }}</textarea>
                </div>

                <button type="submit" class="btn-outline">Send Message</button>
            </form>
        </div>

        <!-- SIDEBAR -->
        <aside class="contact-aside">
            <div class="aside-block">
                <h5>Contact Details</h5>
                <ul>
                    <li><strong>Email:</strong> <a href="mailto:hello@callsoso.com">hello@callsoso.com</a></li>
                    <li><strong>Phone:</strong> <a href="tel:+441234567890">+44 1234 567 890</a></li>
                    <li><strong>Address:</strong> Call Soso HQ, Milton Keynes, UK</li>
                </ul>
            </div>

            <div class="aside-cta">
                <h5>Stay Connected</h5>
                <p>Sign up to receive updates, stories, and impact reports from Call Soso.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="btn-outline light">Join the List</a>
            </div>

            <div class="aside-block">
                <h5>Follow Us</h5>
                <ul>
                    <li><a href="https://www.instagram.com/callsoso_/" target="_blank">Instagram</a></li>
                    <li><a href="https://www.linkedin.com/company/callsoso_/" target="_blank">LinkedIn</a></li>
                </ul>
            </div>
        </aside>

    </section>

    <!-- MICROCOPY -->
    <section class="contact-microcopy">
        <p><em>Virtual hub? Book an audit via our form above.</em></p>
        <p>Interested in volunteering or joining an open call? <a href="{% url 'signup' %}">Sign up</a> to our community.</p>
    </section>

</main>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\home.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Home — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}

<!-- Link homepage-specific CSS -->
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/home.css' %}">

<!-- =========================================================
     HERO — split Material/Edible backdrop, animated slow-pan,
     corner statements, centred wordmark, scroll cue.
========================================================= -->
<header class="hero-main">
    <div class="hero-bg" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="hero-bg-half material" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/hero-material.jpg' %}');"></div>
        <div class="hero-bg-half edible" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/hero-edible.jpg' %}');"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="hero-overlay">
        <p class="corner-note top-left">{{ hero_microcopy.1 }}</p>
        <p class="corner-note top-right">{{ hero_microcopy.2 }}</p>

        <div class="hero-center">
            <h1 class="wordmark">Call Soso</h1>
            <p class="hero-italic">Circularity in Resonance</p>
            {% for line in hero_microcopy %}
                {% if forloop.counter0 != 1 and forloop.counter0 != 2 %}
                    <p class="hero-line">{{ line }}</p>
                {% endif %}
            {% endfor %}
        </div>

        <a href="#intro" class="scroll-cue">
            <span>Scroll to Enter</span>
            <span class="chevron">&#8964;</span>
        </a>
    </div>
</header>

<!-- INTRO STATEMENT -->
<section id="intro" class="intro-statement">
    <p class="eyebrow">Welcome</p>
    <h2>Step inside the Call Soso ecosystem.</h2>
    <p class="lede">A space where circularity becomes movement, meaning, and momentum.</p>
</section>

<!-- MATERIAL LOOP / EDIBLE LOOP SPLIT -->
<section class="loops-split">
    <a href="{% url 'directory:demand_list' %}" class="loop-panel material" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/loop-material.jpg' %}');">
        <span class="eyebrow">The Material Loop</span>
        <h2>Repurposing corporate surplus into new forms, products, and cultural value.</h2>
        <span class="panel-link">Explore Material Loop <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
    </a>
    <a href="{% url 'directory:surplus_list' %}" class="loop-panel edible" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/loop-edible.jpg' %}');">
        <span class="eyebrow">The Edible Loop</span>
        <h2>Reimagining food surplus as nourishment, employment, and civic creativity.</h2>
        <span class="panel-link">Explore Edible Loop <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
    </a>
</section>

<!-- DIRECTORY / SITE INDEX — a genuine sequence through the ecosystem -->
<section class="directory-index">

    <div class="index-row featured-row">
        <a href="{% url 'directory:directory_home' %}" class="index-card featured" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/directory-featured.jpg' %}');">
            <span class="eyebrow">Featured</span>
            <h2>The Exchange Directory</h2>
            <p>A circular resource engine matching surplus with makers.</p>
            <span class="panel-link">Enter Directory <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
        <a href="{% url 'website:about' %}" class="index-card photo" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/index-about.jpg' %}');">
            <span class="index-num">01</span>
            <h3>About</h3>
            <p>The Rooted Manifesto. Our ethos and cultural logic.</p>
            <span class="panel-link small">Read the Manifesto <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="index-row three-up">
        <a href="#" class="index-card photo" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/index-creative-systems.jpg' %}');">
            <span class="index-num">02</span>
            <h3>Creative Systems</h3>
            <p>Consultancy, Audits &amp; Activations.</p>
            <span class="panel-link small">View Creative Systems <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
        <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}" class="index-card photo" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/index-narrative.jpg' %}');">
            <span class="index-num">03</span>
            <h3>The Narrative</h3>
            <p>Impact Stories &amp; Use Cases.</p>
            <span class="panel-link small">Read Stories <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
        <a href="#" class="index-card photo" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/index-living-system.jpg' %}');">
            <span class="index-num">04</span>
            <h3>The Living System</h3>
            <p>Pilots, Workshops &amp; Reuse Builds.</p>
            <span class="panel-link small">See In Action <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
    </div>

    <a href="{% url 'website:impact_tracker' %}" class="index-card wide garden" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/garden.jpg' %}');">
        <div class="garden-main">
            <span class="index-num">05</span>
            <h3>The Garden</h3>
            <p>Gamified Impact Tracker. Track what you plant.</p>
        </div>
        {% if gardens_microcopy %}
        <div class="garden-microcopy">
            {% for line in gardens_microcopy %}
                <p>{{ line }}</p>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
        {% endif %}
        <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span>
    </a>

</section>

<!-- LATEST STORIES -->
<section id="latest-articles" class="latest-articles">
    <p class="eyebrow center">Reading</p>
    <h2 class="section-title">Latest Stories</h2>

    {% if latest_articles %}
        <div class="latest-grid" role="list">
            {% for article in latest_articles %}
                <article class="story-card" role="listitem" aria-labelledby="article-{{ forloop.counter }}"
                          {% if article.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');"{% endif %}>
                    <div class="story-body">
                        <div class="story-meta">
                            {% if article.published_date %}
                                <time datetime="{{ article.published_date }}">
                                    {{ article.published_date|date:"F j, Y" }}
                                </time>
                                &middot;
                            {% endif %}
                            {% if article.author %}
                                <span>{{ article.author.username }}</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>

                        <h3 id="article-{{ forloop.counter }}">
                            {{ article.title|default:"Untitled" }}
                        </h3>

                        <p class="story-excerpt">
                            {% if article.excerpt %}
                                {{ article.excerpt|truncatewords:22 }}
                            {% elif article.summary %}
                                {{ article.summary|truncatewords:22 }}
                            {% elif article.body %}
                                {{ article.body|striptags|truncatewords:22 }}
                            {% else %}
                                No description available.
                            {% endif %}
                        </p>

                        <a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url|default:'#' }}"
                           class="panel-link small"
                           aria-label="Read more about {{ article.title|default:'this story' }}">
                            Read more <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </article>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    {% else %}
        <div class="no-articles">
            <p>
                No stories published yet — come back soon.
                Explore our <a href="{% url 'website:about' %}">approach</a>
                and <a href="{% url 'directory:directory_home' %}">directory</a>.
            </p>
        </div>
    {% endif %}
</section>

<!-- FEATURED RESOURCES -->
{% if featured_resources %}
<section id="featured-resources" class="featured-resources">
    <p class="eyebrow center">Knowledge Center</p>
    <h2 class="section-title">Featured Resources</h2>

    <div class="resource-grid">
        {% for resource in featured_resources %}
            <a href="{{ resource.link|default:'#' }}" class="resource-card"
               {% if resource.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ resource.display_image }}');"{% endif %}>
                <div class="resource-body">
                    <h3>{{ resource.title|default:"Untitled Resource" }}</h3>
                    <p>{{ resource.description|default:""|truncatewords:20 }}</p>
                    <span class="panel-link small">View Resource <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
                </div>
            </a>
        {% endfor %}
    </div>
</section>
{% endif %}

{% endblock %}
```
