# FILE: `website\templates\website\impact_tracker.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Impact Tracker — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<!-- Optional external link if you choose to move styles back to disk later -->
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/impact_tracker.css' %}">

<!-- =========================================================
     EMBEDDED STYLES (Guarantees instant rendering)
========================================================= -->
<style>
    /* Page Container */
    .tracker-page {
        max-width: 1280px;
        margin: 0 auto;
        padding: 60px 40px 120px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        text-align: center;
    }

    /* Centered Editorial Hero (Matching /directory/ & /knowledge/) */
    .tracker-hero {
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 860px;
        margin-bottom: 70px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        text-align: center;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        padding-bottom: 60px;
    }

    .tracker-hero .eyebrow {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        letter-spacing: 0.22em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--grey);
        margin-bottom: 14px;
        display: block;
    }

    .tracker-hero h1 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: clamp(2.6rem, 5vw, 4rem);
        line-height: 1.15;
        margin: 0 0 20px;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    .tracker-hero .lede {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 1.05rem;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: var(--grey);
        max-width: 640px;
        margin: 0 auto 16px;
    }

    .tracker-hero .hero-quote {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-style: italic;
        font-size: 1.15rem;
        color: var(--grey);
        margin: 10px 0 36px;
    }

    /* Controls Strip */
    .tracker-controls {
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 14px;
        width: 100%;
        margin-top: 10px;
    }

    .select-wrapper {
        position: relative;
        display: inline-block;
    }

    .tracker-controls select {
        appearance: none;
        -webkit-appearance: none;
        padding: 12px 38px 12px 20px;
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.75rem;
        font-weight: 500;
        letter-spacing: 0.14em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        background: var(--white);
        color: var(--black);
        outline: none;
        cursor: pointer;
        border-radius: 0;
        transition: border-color 0.25s ease, color 0.25s ease;
    }

    .select-wrapper::after {
        content: "▾";
        position: absolute;
        right: 16px;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translateY(-50%);
        font-size: 0.65rem;
        pointer-events: none;
        color: var(--grey);
    }

    .tracker-controls select:focus,
    .tracker-controls select:hover {
        border-color: var(--orange);
        color: var(--orange);
    }

    /* Buttons */
    .btn-outline {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        border: 1px solid var(--black);
        color: var(--black);
        background: transparent;
        padding: 12px 28px;
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.75rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 0.16em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: all 0.25s ease;
        text-decoration: none;
    }

    .btn-outline:hover {
        background: var(--orange);
        border-color: var(--orange);
        color: var(--white);
    }

    .btn-outline.muted {
        border-color: var(--grey-line);
        color: var(--grey);
    }

    .btn-outline.muted:hover {
        border-color: var(--black);
        color: var(--black);
        background: transparent;
    }

    /* Forest Summary (Matching Directory Counters) */
    .forest-summary {
        width: 100%;
        margin-bottom: 80px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
    }

    .forest-summary .eyebrow {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        letter-spacing: 0.22em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--grey);
        margin-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .forest-summary h2 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 2.2rem;
        margin: 0 0 40px;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    .summary-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(5, 1fr);
        gap: 1px;
        background: var(--grey-line);
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        width: 100%;
    }

    .summary-item {
        background: var(--white);
        padding: 36px 20px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        transition: background 0.25s ease;
    }

    .summary-item:hover {
        background: var(--off-white);
    }

    .summary-item .count {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 2.8rem;
        color: var(--orange);
        line-height: 1;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }

    .summary-item .label {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.72rem;
        letter-spacing: 0.14em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--black);
        font-weight: 600;
    }

    .summary-item .sub {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-style: italic;
        font-size: 0.85rem;
        color: var(--grey);
        margin-top: 4px;
    }

    /* Forest Grid (1px Border Gap & Centered Cards) */
    .forest-grid {
        width: 100%;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));
        gap: 1px;
        background: var(--grey-line);
        border: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
        margin-bottom: 70px;
    }

    .seed-card {
        position: relative;
        background: var(--white);
        padding: 44px 34px;
        min-height: 320px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        text-align: center;
        transition: background 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
        border-top: 3px solid transparent;
    }

    .seed-card:hover {
        background: var(--off-white);
        border-top-color: var(--orange);
        transform: translateY(-4px);
        z-index: 2;
    }

    .card-top {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        width: 100%;
    }

    .stage-num {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.68rem;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 0.18em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--orange);
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }

    .seed-card h4 {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-weight: 500;
        font-size: 1.6rem;
        line-height: 1.25;
        margin: 0 0 14px;
        color: var(--black);
    }

    .seed-card p {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.88rem;
        color: var(--grey);
        line-height: 1.6;
        margin: 0;
    }

    .card-bottom {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        width: 100%;
        margin-top: 28px;
        padding-top: 20px;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--grey-line);
    }

    .planted-date {
        font-family: var(--font-sans);
        font-size: 0.7rem;
        letter-spacing: 0.1em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--grey);
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }

    .stage-dots {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 8px;
    }

    .stage-dots .dot {
        width: 6px;
        height: 6px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--grey-line);
        transition: background 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease;
    }

    .stage-dots .dot.filled {
        background: var(--orange);
        transform: scale(1.25);
    }

    /* Empty State */
    .no-seeds {
        grid-column: 1 / -1;
        background: var(--white);
        padding: 80px 40px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        text-align: center;
        gap: 20px;
    }

    .no-seeds p {
        font-family: var(--font-display);
        font-style: italic;
        font-size: 1.4rem;
        color: var(--grey);
        max-width: 500px;
        margin: 0;
    }

    /* Quick Actions */
    .tracker-actions {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 16px;
        width: 100%;
    }

    /* Responsive Rules */
    @media (max-width: 1024px) {
        .summary-grid {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);
        }
    }

    @media (max-width: 768px) {
        .tracker-page {
            padding: 40px 20px 80px;
        }
        .summary-grid {
            grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
        }
        .summary-item:last-child {
            grid-column: 1 / -1;
        }
        .forest-grid {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
        .tracker-controls {
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: stretch;
        }
        .select-wrapper,
        .tracker-controls select,
        .tracker-controls .btn-outline {
            width: 100%;
            text-align: center;
        }
    }

    @media (max-width: 480px) {
        .summary-grid {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }
        .summary-item:last-child {
            grid-column: auto;
        }
    }
</style>

<main class="tracker-page">

    <!-- HERO SECTION -->
    <header class="tracker-hero">
        <span class="eyebrow">The Garden</span>
        <h1>The Call Soso Forest</h1>
        <p class="lede">Each collaboration plants a new seed. Track our collective growth, resonance, and material impact across the ecosystem.</p>
        <p class="hero-quote">“Hover over any seed to see its resonance lift. Use the controls below to filter and sort the canopy.”</p>

        <div class="tracker-controls">
            <div class="select-wrapper">
                <select id="filter-stage" aria-label="Filter by growth stage">
                    <option value="all">All Growth Stages</option>
                    <option value="1">Stage 1 — Seedling</option>
                    <option value="2">Stage 2 — Sprout</option>
                    <option value="3">Stage 3 — Young Plant</option>
                    <option value="4">Stage 4 — Mature Plant</option>
                    <option value="5">Stage 5 — Blooming Tree</option>
                </select>
            </div>

            <div class="select-wrapper">
                <select id="sort-seeds" aria-label="Sort collaborations">
                    <option value="date-desc">Newest First</option>
                    <option value="date-asc">Oldest First</option>
                    <option value="name-asc">Name A &rarr; Z</option>
                    <option value="name-desc">Name Z &rarr; A</option>
                </select>
            </div>

            <button id="reset-filters" class="btn-outline">Reset Filters</button>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- FOREST SUMMARY (Directory-Style Counters) -->
    <section class="forest-summary">
        <span class="eyebrow">Ecosystem Telemetry</span>
        <h2>Canopy Growth Summary</h2>
        
        <div class="summary-grid">
            <div class="summary-item">
                <span class="count">{{ summary.stage_1|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="label">Seedlings</span>
                <span class="sub">Stage 01</span>
            </div>
            <div class="summary-item">
                <span class="count">{{ summary.stage_2|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="label">Sprouts</span>
                <span class="sub">Stage 02</span>
            </div>
            <div class="summary-item">
                <span class="count">{{ summary.stage_3|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="label">Young Plants</span>
                <span class="sub">Stage 03</span>
            </div>
            <div class="summary-item">
                <span class="count">{{ summary.stage_4|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="label">Mature Plants</span>
                <span class="sub">Stage 04</span>
            </div>
            <div class="summary-item">
                <span class="count">{{ summary.stage_5|default:0 }}</span>
                <span class="label">Blooming Trees</span>
                <span class="sub">Stage 05</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </section>

    <!-- FOREST GRID -->
    <section class="forest-grid" role="list">
        {% for col in collaborations %}
        <article class="seed-card"
                 role="listitem"
                 data-stage="{{ col.growth_stage }}"
                 data-name="{{ col.name }}"
                 data-date="{{ col.planted_date|date:'c' }}">
            <div class="card-top">
                <span class="stage-num">Stage {{ col.growth_stage }}</span>
                <h4>{{ col.name }}</h4>
                <p>{{ col.description|truncatechars:110 }}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="card-bottom">
                <span class="planted-date">Planted {{ col.planted_date|date:"j M Y" }}</span>
                <div class="stage-dots" aria-label="Growth stage {{ col.growth_stage }} of 5">
                    {% for i in "12345" %}
                        <span class="dot {% if forloop.counter <= col.growth_stage %}filled{% endif %}"></span>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>
        {% empty %}
        <div class="no-seeds">
            <p>“No collaborations recorded in the forest yet. Be the first to plant a seed in our circular ecosystem.”</p>
            <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="btn-outline">Initiate Collaboration</a>
        </div>
        {% endfor %}
    </section>

    <!-- QUICK ACTIONS -->
    <section class="tracker-actions">
        <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="btn-outline">Plant a Seed</a>
        <a href="#" class="btn-outline muted">Export Impact Data</a>
    </section>

</main>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.seed-card'));
    const forestGrid = document.querySelector('.forest-grid');

    const filterSelect = document.getElementById('filter-stage');
    const sortSelect = document.getElementById('sort-seeds');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset-filters');

    function renderCards(list) {
        forestGrid.innerHTML = '';
        if (list.length === 0) {
            forestGrid.innerHTML = `
                <div class="no-seeds">
                    <p>“No seeds match the selected growth stage.”</p>
                </div>`;
            return;
        }
        list.forEach(card => forestGrid.appendChild(card));
    }

    function filterAndSort() {
        let filtered = cards;

        const stage = filterSelect.value;
        if (stage !== 'all') {
            filtered = filtered.filter(c => c.dataset.stage === stage);
        }

        const sortVal = sortSelect.value;
        filtered = filtered.slice().sort((a, b) => {
            if (sortVal.startsWith('date')) {
                const aDate = new Date(a.dataset.date);
                const bDate = new Date(b.dataset.date);
                return sortVal.endsWith('desc') ? bDate - aDate : aDate - bDate;
            }
            return sortVal.endsWith('asc')
                ? a.dataset.name.localeCompare(b.dataset.name)
                : b.dataset.name.localeCompare(a.dataset.name);
        });

        renderCards(filtered);
    }

    if (filterSelect && sortSelect && resetBtn) {
        filterSelect.addEventListener('change', filterAndSort);
        sortSelect.addEventListener('change', filterAndSort);
        resetBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            filterSelect.value = 'all';
            sortSelect.value = 'date-desc';
            renderCards(cards);
        });
    }
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\insights.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Insights & Knowledge — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/insights.css' %}">

<!-- HERO -->
<header class="insights-hero" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/insights-hero.jpg' %}');">
    <div class="insights-hero-inner">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Insights &amp; Knowledge</p>
        <h1 class="section-title">Research, opinion, and circular economy innovation.</h1>
        <p class="hero-microcopy">&ldquo;Circularity begins with attention.&rdquo;</p>
    </div>
</header>

<div class="insights-page">

    <!-- CATEGORY FILTER — same pattern as Magazine/Knowledge -->
    <div class="insights-toolbar">
        <nav class="filter-pills">
            <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}" class="filter-pill {% if not selected_category %}active{% endif %}">All</a>
            {% for category in categories %}
            <a href="?category={{ category.slug }}" class="filter-pill {% if category.slug == selected_category %}active{% endif %}">
                {{ category.name }}
                <span class="pill-count">{{ category.story_count }}</span>
            </a>
            {% endfor %}
        </nav>
        {% if selected_category %}
            <p class="browsing-label">Browsing <span>{{ selected_category_name }}</span></p>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <div class="insights-wrapper">

        <!-- MAIN: single editorial grid -->
        <main class="insights-main">
            {% if articles %}
            <div class="articles-grid">
                {% for article in articles %}
                <article class="article-card"
                          {% if article.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');"{% endif %}>
                    <div class="article-body">
                        {% with cats=article.categories.all %}
                            {% if cats %}
                            <span class="article-category">{{ cats.0.name }}</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        {% endwith %}
                        {% if article.published_date %}
                            <div class="article-date">{{ article.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</div>
                        {% endif %}
                        <h4><a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url|default:'#' }}">{{ article.title }}</a></h4>
                        <p class="excerpt">
                            {% if article.excerpt %}{{ article.excerpt|truncatewords:24 }}
                            {% elif article.summary %}{{ article.summary|truncatewords:24 }}
                            {% elif article.body %}{{ article.body|striptags|truncatewords:24 }}
                            {% else %}No description available.{% endif %}
                        </p>
                        <a class="panel-link small" href="{{ article.get_absolute_url|default:'#' }}">
                            Read Article <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </article>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>
            {% else %}
            <p class="empty-state">
                {% if selected_category %}No stories yet in {{ selected_category_name }}.{% else %}No stories published yet.{% endif %}
            </p>
            {% endif %}
        </main>

        <!-- SIDEBAR -->
        {% if popular %}
        <aside class="insights-sidebar">
            <div class="sidebar-section">
                <h3>Recently Published</h3>
                <ul class="hairline-list popular-list">
                    {% for item in popular %}
                    <li>
                        <a href="{{ item.get_absolute_url|default:'#' }}">{{ item.title }}</a>
                        {% if item.published_date %}
                            <span class="date">{{ item.published_date|date:"jS M, Y" }}</span>
                        {% endif %}
                    </li>
                    {% endfor %}
                </ul>
            </div>
        </aside>
        {% endif %}

    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\knowledge_center.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Knowledge Center — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/knowledge_center.css' %}">

<!-- HERO -->
<header class="knowledge-hero" style="background-image: url('{% static 'images/knowledge-hero.jpg' %}');">
    <div class="knowledge-hero-inner">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Knowledge Center</p>
        <h1 class="section-title">Practical guidance for circular teams and creators.</h1>
        <p class="lede center">
            Real-world case studies, tools, and guidance for teams and creators working in the circular economy.
        </p>
        <p class="hero-microcopy">&ldquo;Knowledge is the infrastructure of circularity.&rdquo;</p>
    </div>
</header>

<div class="knowledge-page">

    <!-- CATEGORY FILTER — same pattern as Magazine -->
    <div class="knowledge-toolbar">
        <nav class="filter-pills">
            <a href="{% url 'website:knowledge' %}" class="filter-pill {% if not selected_category %}active{% endif %}">All</a>
            {% for category in categories %}
            <a href="?category={{ category.slug }}" class="filter-pill {% if category.slug == selected_category %}active{% endif %}">
                {{ category.name }}
                <span class="pill-count">{{ category.resource_count }}</span>
            </a>
            {% endfor %}
        </nav>
        {% if selected_category %}
            <p class="browsing-label">Browsing <span>{{ selected_category_name }}</span></p>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <div class="knowledge-wrapper">

        <!-- MAIN -->
        <main class="knowledge-main">

            {% if highlights %}
            <section class="knowledge-section">
                <p class="eyebrow">Highlights</p>
                <div class="resources-grid">
                    {% for resource in highlights %}
                    <article class="resource-card" style="background-image: url('{{ resource.display_image }}');">
                        <div class="resource-body">
                            <span class="resource-badge">{{ resource.resource_type }}</span>
                            {% if resource.published_date %}<div class="resource-date">{{ resource.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</div>{% endif %}
                            <h4>{{ resource.title }}</h4>
                            {% if resource.categories %}<div class="resource-categories">{{ resource.categories|join:" / " }}</div>{% endif %}
                            {% if resource.description %}<p>{{ resource.description|truncatewords:22 }}</p>{% endif %}
                            {% if resource.link %}
                            <a href="{{ resource.link }}" class="panel-link small" target="_blank">Explore <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                    </article>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
            </section>
            {% endif %}

            <section class="knowledge-section">
                <p class="eyebrow">{% if selected_category %}{{ selected_category_name }}{% else %}All Resources{% endif %}</p>
                {% if resources %}
                <div class="resources-grid">
                    {% for resource in resources %}
                    <article class="resource-card" style="background-image: url('{{ resource.display_image }}');">
                        <div class="resource-body">
                            <span class="resource-badge">{{ resource.resource_type }}</span>
                            {% if resource.published_date %}<div class="resource-date">{{ resource.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</div>{% endif %}
                            <h4>{{ resource.title }}</h4>
                            {% if resource.categories %}<div class="resource-categories">{{ resource.categories|join:" / " }}</div>{% endif %}
                            {% if resource.description %}<p>{{ resource.description|truncatewords:20 }}</p>{% endif %}
                            {% if resource.link %}
                            <a href="{{ resource.link }}" class="panel-link small" target="_blank">Read more <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                    </article>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
                {% else %}
                <p class="empty-state">
                    {% if selected_category %}No resources yet in {{ selected_category_name }}.{% else %}No resources published yet.{% endif %}
                </p>
                {% endif %}
            </section>

        </main>

        <!-- SIDEBAR -->
        {% if popular %}
        <aside class="knowledge-sidebar">
            <div class="sidebar-section">
                <h3>Recently Published</h3>
                <ul class="hairline-list popular-list">
                    {% for item in popular %}
                    <li>
                        {% if item.link %}<a href="{{ item.link }}" target="_blank">{{ item.title }}</a>{% else %}<span>{{ item.title }}</span>{% endif %}
                        {% if item.published_date %}<span class="date">{{ item.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</span>{% endif %}
                    </li>
                    {% endfor %}
                </ul>
            </div>
        </aside>
        {% endif %}

    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}The Loops — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/loops.css' %}">

<div class="loops-page">

    <!-- HERO -->
    <header class="loops-hero">
        <div class="hero-row">
            <div class="hero-copy">
                <p class="eyebrow">A Call Soso Framework</p>
                <h1>Realizing and Diverting Your Surplus.</h1>
                <p class="lede">
                    A circular economy framework matching materials, food, strategy, and culture
                    across four connected loops.
                </p>
                <a href="{% url 'website:contact' %}" class="btn-outline">Get an Audit</a>
            </div>
            <div class="hero-visual">
                <span class="hero-visual-label">Call Soso / Loops</span>
            </div>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- LOOP SELECTOR -->
    <section class="loop-selector">
        <div class="selector-heading">
            <p class="eyebrow">Choose Your Loop</p>
            <h2>Four Connected Pathways</h2>
            <p class="lede">
                Pick where surplus, strategy, or culture needs attention first.
            </p>
        </div>

        <div class="loop-grid">
            {% for loop in loops %}
            <a href="{% url loop.cta_url_name %}" class="loop-card {% if loop.highlighted %}highlighted{% endif %}">
                <div class="loop-card-top">
                    <span class="loop-number">{{ loop.number }}</span>
                    <i class="fas {{ loop.icon }}"></i>
                </div>
                <h3>{{ loop.name }}</h3>
                <p>{{ loop.description }}</p>
                <span class="loop-link">{{ loop.cta_label }} <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
            </a>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    </section>

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\magazine.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% now "Y" as current_year %}

{% block title %}Magazine Archive — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/magazine.css' %}">
<main class="magazine-page">

    <!-- MASTHEAD -->
    <header class="magazine-masthead">
        <p class="masthead-eyebrow">Call Soso Presents</p>
        <h1 class="masthead-title">Magazine Archive</h1>
        <p class="masthead-tagline">Circular economy stories, issue by issue.</p>
    </header>

    <!-- CATEGORY FILTER -->
    <div class="magazine-toolbar">
        <nav class="filter-pills">
            <a href="{% url 'website:magazine' %}{% if search_query %}?q={{ search_query|urlencode }}{% endif %}"
               class="filter-pill {% if not selected_category %}active{% endif %}">All</a>
            {% for category in all_categories %}
            <a href="?{% if search_query %}q={{ search_query|urlencode }}&{% endif %}category={{ category.slug }}"
               class="filter-pill {% if category.slug == selected_category %}active{% endif %}">{{ category.name }}</a>
            {% endfor %}
        </nav>
        {% if search_query %}
            <p class="search-query-label">Showing results for &ldquo;{{ search_query }}&rdquo;</p>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    {% if not selected_category %}
    {% if featured_issues %}
    {% with featured_issues.0 as lead %}
    <!-- SPOTLIGHT: the lead featured issue gets the full editorial treatment -->
    <section class="spotlight">
        <a href="{{ lead.get_absolute_url }}" class="spotlight-cover">
            {% if lead.display_image %}<img src="{{ lead.display_image }}" alt="{{ lead.title }}">{% endif %}
        </a>
        <div class="spotlight-content">
            <p class="eyebrow">Latest Issue</p>
            <h2><a href="{{ lead.get_absolute_url }}">{{ lead.title }}</a></h2>
            {% if lead.published_date %}<time class="spotlight-date">{{ lead.published_date|date:"F Y" }}</time>{% endif %}
            <p class="spotlight-excerpt">{{ lead.description|truncatewords:32 }}</p>
            <a href="{{ lead.get_absolute_url }}" class="panel-link small">Read This Issue <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
        </div>
    </section>
    {% endwith %}

    {% if featured_issues|length > 1 %}
    <!-- SHELF: remaining featured issues, standing like covers on a shelf -->
    <section class="shelf-section">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Also Featured</p>
        <div class="shelf">
            {% for issue in featured_issues|slice:"1:" %}
            <article class="shelf-issue">
                <a href="{{ issue.get_absolute_url }}" class="shelf-cover">
                    {% if issue.display_image %}<img src="{{ issue.display_image }}" alt="{{ issue.title }}" loading="lazy">{% endif %}
                </a>
                <h4><a href="{{ issue.get_absolute_url }}">{{ issue.title|truncatewords:6 }}</a></h4>
            </article>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
        <div class="shelf-line"></div>
    </section>
    {% endif %}
    {% endif %}
    {% endif %}

    <!-- ARCHIVE -->
    <div class="magazine-archive">
        {% if selected_category %}
            <p class="browsing-label">Browsing <span>{{ selected_category_name }}</span></p>
        {% endif %}

        {% if issues_by_category %}
            {% for category, issues in issues_by_category.items %}
            <section class="archive-section" id="{{ category|slugify }}">
                <div class="archive-heading">
                    <h2>{{ category }}</h2>
                    <span class="archive-count">{{ issues|length }} Issue{{ issues|length|pluralize }}</span>
                </div>
                {% if issues %}
                <div class="archive-grid">
                    {% for issue in issues %}
                    <article class="issue-card">
                        <a href="{{ issue.get_absolute_url }}" class="issue-cover">
                            {% if issue.display_image %}<img src="{{ issue.display_image }}" alt="{{ issue.title }}" loading="lazy">{% endif %}
                            <span class="issue-badge">No. {{ forloop.counter|stringformat:"02d" }}</span>
                        </a>
                        <div class="issue-caption">
                            <h4><a href="{{ issue.get_absolute_url }}">{{ issue.title }}</a></h4>
                            <span class="issue-date">{{ issue.published_date|date:"M Y" }}</span>
                        </div>
                    </article>
                    {% endfor %}
                </div>
                {% else %}
                <p class="empty-note">No issues yet in {{ category }}.</p>
                {% endif %}
            </section>
            {% endfor %}
        {% else %}
            <div class="empty-state">No issues found.</div>
        {% endif %}
    </div>

    <!-- CLOSING: Popular + Membership side by side -->
    <section class="magazine-closing">
        {% if popular_articles %}
        <div class="closing-block">
            <p class="eyebrow">From the Archive</p>
            <h3>Popular Reads</h3>
            <ul class="hairline-list popular-list">
                {% for article in popular_articles %}
                <li>
                    <a href="{{ article.url }}" class="popular-item">
                        <span class="popular-thumb" {% if article.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');"{% endif %}></span>
                        <span class="popular-title">{{ article.title|truncatewords:8 }}</span>
                    </a>
                </li>
                {% endfor %}
            </ul>
        </div>
        {% endif %}

        <div class="closing-block dark">
            <p class="eyebrow">Membership</p>
            <h3>Become a Member</h3>
            <p>Unlock full magazine access, every issue, every loop.</p>
            <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="btn-outline light">Join Now</a>
        </div>
    </section>

</main>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\news.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}News & Perspectives — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/news.css' %}">

<main class="news-page">

    <!-- HERO -->
    <header class="news-hero">
        <div class="hero-inner">
            <p class="eyebrow center">Insights &amp; Updates</p>
            <h1 class="section-title">News &amp; Perspectives</h1>
            <p class="lede center">
                Stories, research, and reflections from Call Soso and collaborators shaping circular
                systems, material futures, and community impact.
            </p>
        </div>
    </header>

    <!-- FEATURED -->
    {% if featured_articles %}
    {% with featured_articles.0 as feature %}
    <section class="news-feature">

        <article class="feature-card" {% if feature.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ feature.display_image }}');"{% endif %}>
            <div class="feature-content">
                <span class="eyebrow">Featured</span>
                <h2><a href="{{ feature.get_absolute_url }}">{{ feature.title }}</a></h2>
                {% if feature.published_date %}
                    <div class="feature-meta"><time>{{ feature.published_date|date:"jS F Y" }}</time></div>
                {% endif %}
                <p class="excerpt">
                    {% if feature.excerpt %}{{ feature.excerpt|truncatewords:34 }}
                    {% else %}{{ feature.body|striptags|truncatewords:34 }}{% endif %}
                </p>
                <a href="{{ feature.get_absolute_url }}" class="panel-link small">Read full article <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
            </div>
        </article>

        {% if featured_articles|length > 1 %}
        <aside class="feature-secondary">
            <h3 class="secondary-title">More Highlights</h3>
            {% for article in featured_articles|slice:"1:4" %}
            <a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url }}" class="secondary-item" {% if article.display_image %}style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');"{% endif %}>
                <div class="secondary-text">
                    <h4>{{ article.title|truncatewords:9 }}</h4>
                    <span>{{ article.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</span>
                </div>
            </a>
            {% endfor %}
        </aside>
        {% endif %}

    </section>
    {% endwith %}
    {% endif %}

    <!-- FILTER BAR -->
    <section class="news-toolbar">
        <h3>All Articles</h3>
        {% if all_categories %}
        <nav class="category-filters">
            <a href="?" class="filter-tab {% if not selected_category %}active{% endif %}">All</a>
            {% for category in all_categories %}
                <a href="?category={{ category.slug }}" class="filter-tab {% if category.slug == selected_category %}active{% endif %}">
                    {{ category.name }}
                </a>
            {% endfor %}
        </nav>
        {% endif %}
    </section>

    <!-- MAIN LAYOUT -->
    <section class="news-layout">

        <div class="news-stream">
            {% for article in articles %}
            <article class="news-row">
                {% if article.display_image %}
                <a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url }}" class="news-thumb" style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');"></a>
                {% endif %}
                <div class="news-body">
                    <h4><a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url }}">{{ article.title }}</a></h4>
                    <div class="meta">
                        {% if article.published_date %}<span>{{ article.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</span>{% endif %}
                        {% if article.categories.exists %}
                            <span class="dot">&middot;</span>
                            <span class="category">{{ article.categories.first.name }}</span>
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>
                    <p>
                        {% if article.excerpt %}{{ article.excerpt|truncatewords:24 }}
                        {% else %}{{ article.body|striptags|truncatewords:24 }}{% endif %}
                    </p>
                </div>
            </article>
            {% empty %}
            <p class="empty">No articles published yet.</p>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>

        <aside class="news-aside">
            {% if popular_articles %}
            <div class="aside-block">
                <h5>Popular Reads</h5>
                <ul class="hairline-list">
                    {% for article in popular_articles %}
                    <li>
                        <a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url }}">{{ article.title|truncatewords:10 }}</a>
                        <span>{{ article.published_date|date:"j M" }}</span>
                    </li>
                    {% endfor %}
                </ul>
            </div>
            {% endif %}

            <div class="aside-cta">
                <h5>Stay Connected</h5>
                <p>Get essays, updates, and circular economy insights delivered to your inbox.</p>
                <a href="{% url 'signup' %}" class="btn-outline light">Join the List</a>
            </div>
        </aside>

    </section>

    <!-- PAGINATION -->
    {% if is_paginated %}
    <nav class="pagination">
        {% if page_obj.has_previous %}
            <a href="?page={{ page_obj.previous_page_number }}">&larr; Prev</a>
        {% endif %}
        <span class="current">Page {{ page_obj.number }} of {{ page_obj.paginator.num_pages }}</span>
        {% if page_obj.has_next %}
            <a href="?page={{ page_obj.next_page_number }}">Next &rarr;</a>
        {% endif %}
    </nav>
    {% endif %}

</main>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\search_results.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block title %}Search{% if query %}: {{ query }}{% endif %} — Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/search.css' %}">

<div class="search-page">

    <header class="search-header">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Search</p>
        {% if query %}
            <h1 class="section-title">{{ total_results }} result{{ total_results|pluralize }} for &ldquo;{{ query }}&rdquo;</h1>
        {% else %}
            <h1 class="section-title">Search Call Soso</h1>
            <p class="lede center">Find issues, stories, and services from across the site.</p>
        {% endif %}
    </header>

    {% if query and total_results == 0 %}
        <div class="empty-state">
            <p>No results for &ldquo;{{ query }}&rdquo;.</p>
            <p class="empty-sub">Try a different word, or browse <a href="{% url 'website:magazine' %}">the Magazine</a>,
               <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}">Stories</a>, or <a href="{% url 'website:knowledge' %}">Services</a> directly.</p>
        </div>
    {% endif %}

    {% if issues %}
    <section class="results-section">
        <div class="results-heading">
            <h2>Magazine Issues</h2>
            <a href="{% url 'website:magazine' %}?q={{ query|urlencode }}" class="panel-link small">See All <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
        </div>
        <div class="results-grid">
            {% for issue in issues %}
            <article class="result-card" style="background-image: url('{{ issue.display_image }}');">
                <div class="result-body">
                    <span class="result-type">Issue</span>
                    <h3><a href="{{ issue.get_absolute_url }}">{{ issue.title }}</a></h3>
                    {% if issue.published_date %}<span class="result-meta">{{ issue.published_date|date:"M Y" }}</span>{% endif %}
                </div>
            </article>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    </section>
    {% endif %}

    {% if articles %}
    <section class="results-section">
        <div class="results-heading">
            <h2>Stories</h2>
            <a href="{% url 'website:insights' %}" class="panel-link small">See All <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
        </div>
        <div class="results-grid">
            {% for article in articles %}
            <article class="result-card" style="background-image: url('{{ article.display_image }}');">
                <div class="result-body">
                    <span class="result-type">Story</span>
                    <h3><a href="{{ article.get_absolute_url }}">{{ article.title }}</a></h3>
                    {% if article.published_date %}<span class="result-meta">{{ article.published_date|date:"j M Y" }}</span>{% endif %}
                    <p class="result-excerpt">
                        {% if article.excerpt %}{{ article.excerpt|truncatewords:18 }}
                        {% else %}{{ article.body|striptags|truncatewords:18 }}{% endif %}
                    </p>
                </div>
            </article>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    </section>
    {% endif %}

    {% if resources %}
    <section class="results-section">
        <div class="results-heading">
            <h2>Services &amp; Resources</h2>
            <a href="{% url 'website:knowledge' %}" class="panel-link small">See All <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
        </div>
        <div class="results-grid">
            {% for resource in resources %}
            <article class="result-card" style="background-image: url('{{ resource.display_image }}');">
                <div class="result-body">
                    <span class="result-type">Service</span>
                    <h3>{{ resource.title }}</h3>
                    <p class="result-excerpt">{{ resource.description|truncatewords:18 }}</p>
                    {% if resource.link %}
                    <a href="{{ resource.link }}" class="panel-link small" target="_blank">View <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></a>
                    {% endif %}
                </div>
            </article>
            {% endfor %}
        </div>
    </section>
    {% endif %}

</div>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\templates\website\support.html`

```html
{% extends "website/base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% block title %}Support Call Soso{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'css/support.css' %}">

<div class="support-page">

    <header class="support-hero">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Support Us</p>
        <h1 class="section-title">Support Call Soso</h1>
        <p class="lede center">Your contributions help us grow the circular economy community.</p>
    </header>

    <!-- FUNDING OPTIONS -->
    <section class="funding-options">
        <a href="https://www.kickstarter.com" target="_blank" class="fund-card">
            <span class="eyebrow">Kickstarter</span>
            <h3>Back the Campaign</h3>
            <span class="panel-link small">Visit Kickstarter <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
        <a href="https://www.buymeacoffee.com" target="_blank" class="fund-card">
            <span class="eyebrow">One-Off Support</span>
            <h3>Buy Us a Coffee</h3>
            <span class="panel-link small">Visit Buy Me a Coffee <span class="arrow">&rarr;</span></span>
        </a>
    </section>

    <!-- CONTRIBUTIONS -->
    <section class="contributions">
        <p class="eyebrow center">Community</p>
        <h2 class="section-title">Recent Contributions</h2>

        <ul class="contrib-list">
            {% for contrib in contributions %}
            <li class="contrib-item" data-amount="{{ contrib.amount|default:0 }}">
                <span class="contrib-name">{{ contrib.contributor_name|default:contrib.user.username|default:"Anonymous" }}</span>
                <span class="contrib-detail">
                    {% if contrib.amount %}
                        donated &pound;{{ contrib.amount }}
                    {% else %}
                        contributed via {{ contrib.get_source_display }}
                    {% endif %}
                    on {{ contrib.date|date:"j M Y" }}
                </span>
            </li>
            {% empty %}
            <li class="contrib-item empty">No contributions yet. Be the first to support us.</li>
            {% endfor %}
        </ul>
    </section>

</div>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const items = document.querySelectorAll('.contrib-item:not(.empty)');
    items.forEach(item => {
        item.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            const amount = parseFloat(item.dataset.amount) || 0;
            item.classList.add('is-hovered');
            item.classList.toggle('major-gift', amount > 50);
        });
        item.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            item.classList.remove('is-hovered', 'major-gift');
        });
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

# FILE: `website\urls.py`

```python
from django.urls import path
from . import views

app_name = "website"  # Keep this for your main website pages

# Core + namespaced pages
urlpatterns = [
    # Core pages
    path('', views.home, name='home'),
    path('about/', views.about, name='about'),
    path('contact/', views.contact, name='contact'),

    # News / Articles
    path('news/', views.news, name='news'),
    path('news/<slug:slug>/', views.article_detail, name='article_detail'),

    # Content / Knowledge
    path('insights/', views.insights, name='insights'),
    path('knowledge/', views.knowledge_center, name='knowledge'),
    path('categories/', views.categories, name='categories'),
    path('magazine/', views.magazine, name='magazine'),

    # Features (some render templates in directory app, but routed via website)
    path('impact-tracker/', views.impact_tracker, name='impact_tracker'),
    path('support/', views.support, name='support'),
    path('loops/', views.loops_detail, name='loops_detail'),
    path('tiers/', views.tiers, name='tiers'),
    path('search/', views.search, name='search'),
]

# Auth URLs (outside namespace for simplicity)
auth_urlpatterns = [
    path('signup/', views.signup_view, name='signup'),
    path('login/', views.login_view, name='login'),
    path('logout/', views.logout_view, name='logout'),
]
```

```python
from datetime import date
from collections import OrderedDict
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect, get_object_or_404, resolve_url
from django.core.mail import send_mail
from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import login, logout
from django.core.paginator import Paginator
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.contrib.auth.forms import UserCreationForm, AuthenticationForm
from django.contrib import messages
from django.db.models import Count
from django.db.models import Q
from .models import (
    Collaboration,
    Contribution,
    FoundersList,
    Article,
    Resource,
    Category,
    MagazineIssue,
    PopularArticle,
)

# ---------------------------
# Magazine / Popular Data
# ---------------------------
MAGAZINE_ISSUES = [
    {"title": "Circular Autumn 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Summer 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Spring 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Winter 2024", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Autumn 2024", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Summer 2024", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular Spring 2024", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular November/December 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular September/October 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular July/August 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular May/June 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular March/April 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular January/February 2023", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Circular November/December 2022", "url": "#"},
]

POPULAR_ARTICLES = [
    {"title": "WRAP shines a spotlight on toothpaste tubes this Recycle Week", "date": "September 23, 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "Encyclis to develop ‘UK’s first’ carbon capture project for EfW", "date": "September 25, 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "EU adopts new rules to reduce textile and food waste", "date": "September 10, 2025", "url": "#"},
    {"title": "UK post-consumer plastic exports rely on ‘a broken system full of criminality and death’, investigation finds", "date": "September 26, 2025", "url": "#"},
]

# ---------------------------
# Home View
# ---------------------------
def home(request):
    hero_microcopy = [
        "Every material has a second life.",
        "Surplus isn’t waste, it’s unrealised potential.",
        "Culture forms from what we choose to value.",
        "Circularity begins with attention.",
        "Creativity is a form of infrastructure.",
    ]
    gardens_microcopy = [
        "Ideas grow wherever they’re planted.",
        "Every material carries a history.",
        "Circularity begins with attention.",
    ]

    latest_articles = Article.objects.filter(is_published=True).order_by("-published_date", "-created_at")[:3]
    featured_resources = Resource.objects.filter(published=True, is_featured=True).order_by("-created_at")[:3]

    if request.method == "POST":
        email = request.POST.get("email")
        if email:
            FoundersList.objects.get_or_create(email=email)
            messages.success(request, "Thanks for joining the Call Soso founders list.")
            return redirect("website:home")

    context = {
        "hero_microcopy": hero_microcopy,
        "gardens_microcopy": gardens_microcopy,
        "latest_articles": latest_articles,
        "featured_resources": featured_resources,
    }

    return render(request, "website/home.html", context)


# ---------------------------
# About
# ---------------------------
def about(request):
    return render(request, 'website/about.html')

# ---------------------------
# Contact
# ---------------------------
def contact(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        name = request.POST.get('name')
        email = request.POST.get('email')
        organization = request.POST.get('organization')
        inquiry_type = request.POST.get('inquiry_type')
        message = request.POST.get('message')

        if email and message:
            send_mail(
                f"Contact Inquiry: {inquiry_type} from {name}",
                f"From: {name}\nEmail: {email}\nOrganization: {organization}\n\nMessage:\n{message}",
                settings.DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,
                [settings.CONTACT_EMAIL],
                fail_silently=False,
            )
            messages.success(request, "Your message has been sent successfully.")
            return redirect('website:contact')
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Please provide both your email and a message.")
    return render(request, 'website/contact.html')

# ---------------------------
# News View
# ---------------------------
def news(request):
    # Fetch all published articles
    articles_qs = Article.objects.filter(is_published=True).order_by("-published_date", "-created_at")

    # FIXED: the category filter pills in the template built a
    # ?category=<slug> URL, but this view never read or applied it —
    # clicking a category silently did nothing.
    category_slug = request.GET.get("category", "").strip()
    if category_slug:
        articles_qs = articles_qs.filter(categories__slug=category_slug)

    # Featured and popular
    featured_articles = articles_qs.filter(is_featured=True)[:3]
    popular_articles = articles_qs[:4]

    # Pagination
    paginator = Paginator(articles_qs, 5)
    page_number = request.GET.get("page")
    page_obj = paginator.get_page(page_number)

    # All categories for filter UI
    all_categories = Category.objects.all()

    context = {
        "articles": page_obj.object_list,
        "featured_articles": featured_articles,
        "popular_articles": popular_articles,
        "page_obj": page_obj,
        "is_paginated": page_obj.has_other_pages(),
        "all_categories": all_categories,
        "selected_category": category_slug,  # FIXED: template needs this to highlight the active pill
    }

    return render(request, "website/news.html", context)


# ---------------------------
# Article Detail View
# ---------------------------
def article_detail(request, slug):
    # Fetch the requested article
    article = get_object_or_404(Article, slug=slug, is_published=True)

    # Fetch related articles: same categories, exclude current article
    related_articles = Article.objects.filter(
        is_published=True,
        categories__in=article.categories.all()
    ).exclude(pk=article.pk).distinct().order_by('-published_date')[:4]

    context = {
        'article': article,
        'related_articles': related_articles,
    }
    return render(request, 'website/article_detail.html', context)

# ---------------------------
# Sitewide Search
# ---------------------------
def search(request):
    """
    Single search box in the header searches across:
      - Magazine Issues (title, description)
      - Stories / Articles (title, excerpt, summary, body)
      - Services / Resources (title, description)
    Replaces the separate per-page search bars (e.g. the old Magazine
    page search) with one consistent, sitewide search experience.
    """
    query = request.GET.get("q", "").strip()

    issues = []
    articles = []
    resources = []

    if query:
        issues = (
            MagazineIssue.objects.filter(is_published=True)
            .filter(Q(title__icontains=query) | Q(description__icontains=query))
            .order_by("-published_date")[:12]
        )

        articles = (
            Article.objects.filter(is_published=True)
            .filter(
                Q(title__icontains=query)
                | Q(excerpt__icontains=query)
                | Q(summary__icontains=query)
                | Q(body__icontains=query)
            )
            .order_by("-published_date")[:12]
        )

        resources = (
            Resource.objects.filter(published=True)
            .filter(Q(title__icontains=query) | Q(description__icontains=query))
            .order_by("-created_at")[:12]
        )

    total_results = len(issues) + len(articles) + len(resources)

    context = {
        "query": query,
        "issues": issues,
        "articles": articles,
        "resources": resources,
        "total_results": total_results,
    }
    return render(request, "website/search_results.html", context)

# Directory Home (private)

@login_required(login_url=settings.LOGIN_URL)
def directory_home(request):
    return render(request, 'directory/index.html')

# ---------------------------
# Insights
# ---------------------------
def insights(request):
    """
    Insights / Stories page.

    Rebuilt to match Magazine/Knowledge: server-side ?category=slug
    filtering via filter-pills, single editorial grid instead of grouped
    category sections. No more JS-based show/hide.
    """

    # Categories, annotated with a live count of published articles
    # (used for "18" style counts on each filter pill)
    categories = Category.objects.annotate(
        story_count=Count("article", filter=Q(article__is_published=True), distinct=True)
    ).order_by("name")

    selected_category = request.GET.get("category")

    articles = Article.objects.filter(is_published=True).order_by(
        "-published_date", "-created_at"
    )

    if selected_category:
        articles = articles.filter(categories__slug=selected_category).distinct()

    # Popular / recently published sidebar — top 5 regardless of filter
    popular = Article.objects.filter(is_published=True).order_by(
        "-published_date", "-created_at"
    )[:5]

    selected_category_name = None
    if selected_category:
        cat = Category.objects.filter(slug=selected_category).first()
        if cat:
            selected_category_name = cat.name

    context = {
        "categories": categories,
        "articles": articles,
        "popular": popular,
        "selected_category": selected_category,
        "selected_category_name": selected_category_name,
    }

    return render(request, "website/insights.html", context)


# ---------------------------
# Knowledge Center
# ---------------------------
def knowledge_center(request):
    """
    Knowledge Center
 
    Features
    --------
    • Category filtering (?category=slug) via filter-pills, same pattern as Magazine
    • Featured resources (highlights)
    • Recently published sidebar
    """
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Categories, annotated with a live count of published resources
    # (used to show "14 Resources" style counts on each filter pill)
    # ---------------------------------------
    categories = Category.objects.annotate(
        resource_count=Count("resource", filter=Q(resource__published=True), distinct=True)
    ).order_by("name")
 
    # Selected category from URL
    selected_category = request.GET.get("category")
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Base queryset
    # ---------------------------------------
    resources_qs = (
        Resource.objects.filter(published=True)
        .prefetch_related("categories")
        .order_by("-created_at")
    )
 
    # Filter by category if supplied
    if selected_category:
        resources_qs = resources_qs.filter(
            categories__slug=selected_category
        ).distinct()
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Featured + Regular Resources
    # ---------------------------------------
    highlights = []
    resources = []
 
    for resource in resources_qs:
        category_list = list(resource.categories.all())
 
        resource_data = {
            "title": resource.title,
            "published_date": resource.created_at,
            "description": resource.description,
            "link": resource.link,
            "display_image": resource.display_image,
            "resource_type": resource.get_resource_type_display(),
            "categories": [c.name for c in category_list],
        }
 
        if resource.is_featured:
            highlights.append(resource_data)
        else:
            resources.append(resource_data)
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Sidebar
    # ---------------------------------------
    popular = []
 
    for resource in (
        Resource.objects.filter(published=True)
        .order_by("-created_at")[:5]
    ):
        popular.append({
            "title": resource.title,
            "published_date": resource.created_at,
            "link": resource.link,
        })
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Selected category name (for the "Browsing X" label)
    # ---------------------------------------
    selected_category_name = None
 
    if selected_category:
        category = Category.objects.filter(slug=selected_category).first()
        if category:
            selected_category_name = category.name
 
    # ---------------------------------------
    # Context
    # ---------------------------------------
    context = {
        "categories": categories,
 
        "highlights": highlights,
        "resources": resources,
        "popular": popular,
 
        "selected_category": selected_category,
        "selected_category_name": selected_category_name,
    }
 
    return render(
        request,
        "website/knowledge_center.html",
        context,
    )
 


# ---------------------------
# Categories
# ---------------------------
def categories(request):
    """
    Categories hub — shows every real Category with counts of how much
    content exists under it across Stories (Articles), Services
    (Resources), and Magazine Issues, each linking into the right place.
    """
    all_categories = Category.objects.all().order_by("name")
 
    category_data = []
    for cat in all_categories:
        category_data.append({
            "category": cat,
            "story_count": cat.article_set.filter(is_published=True).count(),
            "service_count": cat.resource_set.filter(published=True).count(),
            "issue_count": cat.magazineissue_set.filter(is_published=True).count(),
        })
 
    context = {"category_data": category_data}
    return render(request, "website/categories.html", context)

# ---------------------------
# Magazine
# ---------------------------

def magazine(request):
    query = request.GET.get("q", "").strip()
    category_slug = request.GET.get("category", "").strip()

    # 1. Base queryset with optimization
    issues = (
        MagazineIssue.objects.filter(is_published=True)
        .prefetch_related("categories")
        .order_by("-published_date", "-created_at")
    )

    # 2. Apply search filter if present
    if query:
        issues = issues.filter(title__icontains=query)

    # 3. Separate Featured Issues
    featured_issues = issues.filter(is_featured=True)[:4]
    regular_issues = issues.exclude(
        id__in=[issue.id for issue in featured_issues]
    )

    # 4. Determine which categories to display
    if category_slug:
        active_categories = Category.objects.filter(slug=category_slug)
    else:
        active_categories = Category.objects.all().order_by("name")

    # 5. Group regular issues into sections by Category
    issues_by_category = OrderedDict()
    for cat in active_categories:
        cat_issues = regular_issues.filter(categories=cat).distinct()
        # Display section if it has issues OR if the user explicitly clicked its filter
        if cat_issues.exists() or category_slug:
            issues_by_category[cat] = cat_issues

    # Handle uncategorized issues when viewing "All Issues"
    if not category_slug:
        uncategorized = regular_issues.filter(categories__isnull=True)
        if uncategorized.exists():
            issues_by_category["Uncategorized"] = uncategorized

    # 6. Sidebar data & metadata
    all_categories = Category.objects.all().order_by("name")
    selected_category_obj = active_categories.first() if category_slug else None
    popular_articles = PopularArticle.objects.all()[:5]

    context = {
        "featured_issues": featured_issues,
        "issues_by_category": (
            issues_by_category
        ),  # <-- Grouped sections dictionary
        "popular_articles": popular_articles,
        "search_query": query,
        "all_categories": all_categories,
        "selected_category": category_slug,
        "selected_category_name": (
            selected_category_obj.name if selected_category_obj else "All"
        ),
    }

    return render(request, "website/magazine.html", context)

# ---------------------------
# Signup / Login / Logout
# ---------------------------
def signup_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        form = UserCreationForm(request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            user = form.save()
            login(request, user)
            messages.success(request, "Welcome! Your account has been created.")
            return redirect('directory:directory_home')
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Please correct the errors below.")
    else:
        form = UserCreationForm()
    return render(request, 'website/signup.html', {'form': form})

def login_view(request):
    next_page = request.GET.get('next') or 'directory:directory_home'

    if request.method == "POST":
        form = AuthenticationForm(request, data=request.POST)
        if form.is_valid():
            user = form.get_user()
            login(request, user)
            messages.success(request, f"Welcome back, {user.username}!")
            return redirect(resolve_url(next_page))
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Invalid username or password.")
    else:
        form = AuthenticationForm()
    return render(request, 'website/login.html', {'form': form})



def logout_view(request):
    logout(request)
    messages.info(request, "You have been logged out.")
    return redirect('website:home')

# ---------------------------
# Impact Tracker / Support / Loops / Tiers
# ---------------------------

@login_required(login_url=settings.LOGIN_URL)
def impact_tracker(request):
    collaborations = Collaboration.objects.all().order_by('-planted_date')

    # FIXED: the template needs summary.stage_1..stage_5 for the Forest Growth
    # Summary bars, but the original view never computed or passed this —
    # the bars were rendering with data-count="" (undefined), which the JS
    # parsed as NaN and multiplied into "NaNpx" widths.
    stage_counts = {row['growth_stage']: row['count'] for row in
                    Collaboration.objects.values('growth_stage').annotate(count=Count('id'))}
    summary = {f'stage_{i}': stage_counts.get(i, 0) for i in range(1, 6)}

    return render(request, 'website/impact_tracker.html', {
        'collaborations': collaborations,
        'summary': summary,
    })

@login_required(login_url=settings.LOGIN_URL)
def support(request):
    contributions = Contribution.objects.all().order_by('-date')
    return render(request, 'website/support.html', {'contributions': contributions})
```
